Result: 识别出两种互补的压力介导机制：1）Stokes应变反转时，压力-应变再分配Π_uu对流向速度方差施加显著耗散，而温度方差方程无此对应项；2）准稳态平台阶段，压力-温度梯度相关Π_vθ优先增强壁面法向标量通量。两种机制均集中在反转和平台阶段而非Stokes层穿透最大处。

Conclusion: 准稳态相位的持续时间是控制非相似热传输增强的关键参数，解释了穿透深度增加并不产生更大非相似性的悖论。

Abstract: Spanwise wall oscillation can enhance convective heat transfer disproportionately to its drag penalty, a departure from the Reynolds analogy termed dissimilar heat transfer (DHT). The companion study of Gu'erin et al. (2026) established that an optimised quasi-plateau waveform attains an analogy factor $\overline{A}n \approx 1.09$ at $Pr = 1$ and attributed this preferential thermal enhancement to the absence of a pressure-strain redistribution channel in the temperature variance equation, but the mechanism had not been quantitatively verified. The present study addresses this gap through phase-resolved variance transport budget analysis from direct numerical simulation of turbulent channel flow at $Reτ= 200$, $Pr = 1$. Two complementary pressure-mediated mechanisms are identified. At the Stokes-strain reversal, the pressure-strain redistribution $Π_{uu}$ imposes a pronounced drain on the streamwise velocity variance with no counterpart in the temperature variance equation: the divergence-free constraint redistributes momentum variance among velocity components but has no scalar analogue. During the quasi-steady plateau phases, the pressure-temperature-gradient correlation $Π_{vθ}$ preferentially enhances the wall-normal scalar flux relative to the momentum flux. The concentration of both mechanisms within the reversal and plateau phases, rather than at the Stokes-layer penetration maxima, identifies the duration of the quasi-steady phases as the controlling parameter for DHT enhancement, resolving the paradox whereby increased penetration depth does not produce increased dissimilarity.

</details>


### [82] [Patched Flow Matching: Generative Wall-Pressure Reconstruction Beyond Training-Domain Scales from Sparse Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22084)
*Meet Hemant Parikh,Yi Liu,Jian-Xun Wang*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出Patched Flow Matching (Patched FM)生成式框架，通过融合短域DNS和稀疏传感器数据，在比训练域大4倍的域上以低至0.25%的传感器覆盖率重建湍流壁面压力场，并实现雷诺数高效迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 湍流壁面边界层中完整的壁面压力波数谱需要同时获取粘性尺度的高波数内容和外层的低波数内容，而短域直接数值模拟(DNS)和稀疏实验测量均无法单独满足这一需求。

Method: 提出Patched Flow Matching框架：1) 通过分块局部先验学习内尺度壁面压力统计特性；2) 推理时通过无训练后验采样同化稀疏传感器数据；3) 采用分块加性分解解耦生成先验与全局域尺寸；4) 基于内尺度坐标的分块先验利用高波数壁面压力统计的近似雷诺数不变性，通过分层迁移学习扩展至高雷诺数。

Result: 在Re_τ=180、500、1000的可压缩槽道DNS中，Patched FM在比训练配置大4倍的域上(16πδ vs 4πδ)，以低至0.25%的传感器覆盖率重建全分辨率壁面压力场，高保真恢复短域DNS无法获取的低波数谱内容。迁移学习仅需500个短域快照(基础训练数据的2.5%)，成本远低于从头训练。

Conclusion: Patched FM成功融合互补数据源实现湍流壁面压力场的超分辨率重建与域外推，内尺度作为物理先验是实现数据高效雷诺数迁移的关键，零样本泛化实验进一步验证了该结论。

Abstract: Characterizing the complete wall-pressure spectrum in turbulent wall-bounded flows requires simultaneous access to the viscous-scale high-wavenumber content and the outer-layer low-wavenumber content -- a requirement that neither short-domain direct numerical simulation (DNS) nor sparse experimental measurements alone can satisfy. We propose Patched Flow Matching (Patched FM), a generative framework that fuses these two complementary sources by learning a patch-local prior over inner-scaled wall-pressure statistics from short-domain DNS and assimilating sparse sensor measurements at inference time through training-free posterior sampling. The patch-additive decomposition of the flow matching vector field decouples the generative prior from the global domain size, enabling reconstruction on domains arbitrarily larger than the training configuration. By expressing the patch prior in inner-scaled coordinates, where high-wavenumber wall-pressure statistics are approximately Reynolds-number invariant, the framework extends to higher Reynolds numbers through hierarchical transfer learning with as few as $500$ short-domain snapshots ($2.5\%$ of the base training data) at a fraction of the scratch-training cost. Applied to compressible channel-flow DNS at $Re_τ= 180$, $500$, and $1000$, Patched FM reconstructs full-resolution wall-pressure fields on a domain four times larger than the training configuration ($L_x^L = 16πδ$ versus $L_x^S = 4πδ$) from sensor coverage as low as $0.25\%$, recovering the low-wavenumber spectral content inaccessible to short-domain DNS with high fidelity in both streamwise and spanwise directions. Zero-shot generalization to unseen Reynolds numbers and ablation studies further confirm the role of inner scaling as a physical prerequisite for data-efficient Reynolds-number transfer.

</details>


### [83] [Bi-stable Nonlinear Energy Sinks (BNESs) for Response Mitigation and Drag Reduction of Subsea Cables Undergoing Vortex-induced Vibrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22638)
*Anargyros Michaloliakos,Ryan Davies,Matthew Hall,Alexander F. Vakakis*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出利用双稳态非线性吸振器（BNES）被动抑制海底动态动力电缆涡激振动（VIV），通过MoorDyn开源库的三维时域仿真和数据驱动优化，验证了BNES能有效降低电缆振动幅值、累积能量摄入和拖曳能，并首次证明被动非线性附件可同时减少能量摄入与拖曳放大效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海底动态动力电缆在复杂流场中易发生涡激振动（VIV），导致疲劳损伤和能量耗散问题。现有被动抑制方法对非平稳VIV的适应性和鲁棒性不足，需要开发更有效的被动减振技术。

Method: 基于MoorDyn开源库建立全三维时域框架，模拟电缆动力学、Morison型水动力、非线性减振机制及时空变化流场；将双稳态非线性吸振器（BNES）集成到电缆模型中；通过数据驱动优化，在多流速剖面下采样BNES设计空间，寻找最优配置。

Result: 适当调谐的BNES显著降低电缆峰-峰振动幅值；有效减少VIV诱导的累积能量摄入和拖曳能；时频小波分析揭示了靶向非线性能量转移，将低频高幅模态能量散射到高频低幅模态，通过水动力阻尼和结构损耗实现快速耗散。

Conclusion: BNES能在真实非稳态工况下为海底电缆提供有效且鲁棒的VIV抑制，首次证明被动非线性附件可同时减少能量摄入和拖曳放大效应，对短期和长期疲劳具有潜在益处，并为未来考虑波流联合载荷、平台运动及疲劳寿命评估的研究提供了基础。

Abstract: A methodology for passive mitigation of vortex-induced vibrations (VIVs) in subsea dynamic power cables is developed using optimized, strongly nonlinear bi-stable mass-spring-damper attachments, termed bi-stable nonlinear energy sinks (BNESs), within the open-source MoorDyn library. A fully three-dimensional time-domain framework captures cable dynamics, Morison-type hydrodynamic forcing, nonlinear vibration-mitigation mechanisms, and spatially and temporally varying currents. The BNESs are consistently integrated into the cable model, allowing treatment of highly non-stationary VIVs. A data-driven optimization study samples the BNES design space across multiple current profiles and shows that properly tuned configurations substantially reduce peak-to-peak cable vibration amplitudes. The BNESs also produce significant and robust reductions in cumulative VIV-induced energy intake from the surrounding flow and in drag energy. To the authors' knowledge, passive nonlinear attachments are shown for the first time to reduce both energy intake and drag amplification in a subsea cable, with potential benefits for short- and long-term fatigue. Time-frequency wavelet analysis reveals targeted nonlinear energy transfers and scattering from dominant high-amplitude, low-frequency cable modes to lower-amplitude, higher-frequency modes. This modal redistribution promotes rapid dissipation through hydrodynamic damping and internal structural losses, explaining the simultaneous reductions in vibration amplitude and cumulative energy intake. The results demonstrate that BNESs can provide effective and robust VIV mitigation for subsea power cables under realistic unsteady operating conditions and motivate future studies involving combined current-wave loading, platform-induced motion, and fatigue-life assessment.

</details>


### [84] [A Conservative Time-Accurate Local Time-Stepping DG Scheme Based on a Weakly Compressible Model for Unsteady Low-Mach-Number Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22820)
*Shihao Liu,Keli Zhang,Yuning Luan,Kai Liu*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 提出一种基于弱可压缩公式的保守高阶间断伽辽金方法，结合时间精确局部时间步进，用于模拟低马赫数非定常流动与气动声学问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低马赫数非定常流动的数值模拟中，传统不可压求解器需全局求解压力泊松方程，破坏了局部性与守恒性；而可压缩格式在低速下存在刚性问题。本文旨在结合二者优势，发展兼具局部性、守恒性与时间精确性的高效方法。

Method: 基于弱可压缩公式（压力仅为密度的函数），空间离散采用强形式节点DGSEM（GLL点），无黏通量使用针对弱可压缩系统的双稀疏散射近似黎曼求解器，黏性项采用IIPG方法。时间积分采用连续延拓龙格-库塔（CERK）格式构建单元局部预测多项式，面通量分裂为内部贡献与公共贡献，保持离散求和分部抵消与守恒通量交换。

Result: 该方法在保持保守性的同时实现了时间精确的局部时间步进，适用于低马赫数非定常流动与气动声学模拟，克服了全局压力泊松方程带来的非局部性限制。

Conclusion: 所提方法为低马赫数非定常流动提供了一种兼具可压缩格式守恒性与不可压格式效率的新途径，通过局部时间步进与弱可压缩公式的结合，在保证精度的同时显著提升计算效率。

Abstract: This paper presents a conservative high-order discontinuous Galerkin (DG) method featuring time-accurate local time stepping for simulating low-Mach-number unsteady flows, based on a weakly compressible formulation. In this model, pressure is defined solely as a function of density, eliminating the need for a global pressure Poisson equation typical of incompressible solvers while preserving the locality and conservation of compressible schemes. This makes it suitable for low-speed unsteady flows and aeroacoustics. The spatial discretization uses a strong-form nodal DG spectral element method (DGSEM) on Gauss-Lobatto-Legendre points. Inviscid fluxes are handled by numerical fluxes tailored to the weakly compressible system; specifically, a two-rarefaction approximate Riemann solver is developed for the constant-sound-speed barotropic equation of state. Viscous terms employ the incomplete interior penalty Galerkin (IIPG) method. For time integration, a continuous extension Runge-Kutta (CERK) scheme constructs cell-local predictor polynomials for continuous-in-time volume reconstructions. Face fluxes are split into interior and common contributions: the former matches the volume quadrature, while the latter uses piecewise Gaussian quadrature from continuous predictors. This split preserves discrete summation-by-parts cancellation and ensures conservative inter-element flux exchange.

</details>


### [85] [Non-monotonic variations in pressure drop and chaos in viscoelastic fluid flows through an ordered microporous medium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22848)
*A. Chauhan,C. Sasmal*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过数值模拟研究了粘弹性流体在交错排列微柱多孔介质中的流动，提出双折射应力 strands（由聚合物分子拉伸排列产生）是控制混沌行为和压降非单调变化的主导机制。在低Weissenberg数时应力 strands逐渐发展，超过临界值后强烈波动引发混沌，而在更高Weissenberg数时strands增强并连接相邻微柱，使流动重新组织为近稳态有序状态。压降的平均分量单调增加，但脉动分量非单调变化，导致总压降呈现非单调趋势。混沌行为的非单调性还强烈依赖于多孔介质的固相体积分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期实验发现粘弹性流体在微孔介质中流动时，弹性湍流引发的混沌行为和压降呈现非单调变化，但其背后的物理机制尚不明确。本研究旨在通过数值模拟探究并假设控制这些复杂流动行为的物理机制。

Method: 采用数值模拟方法，研究粘弹性流体在由交错排列圆柱形微柱构成的有序微孔介质中的流动行为。

Result: 1）双折射应力 strands是控制混沌和压降非单调变化的主导机制；2）随Weissenberg数增加，应力strands经历"发展→强烈波动→连接重组"的演化过程；3）压降的非单调性源于脉动分量的非单调变化，而平均分量单调增加；4）固相体积分数影响混沌行为的临界Weissenberg数及非单调性范围。

Conclusion: 双折射应力 strands的形成、波动和互联是导致弹性湍流混沌行为和压降非单调变化的根本物理机制，且该行为受多孔介质几何参数调控。

Abstract: Several recent experimental studies have revealed non-monotonic variations in elastic turbulence-induced chaotic flow behaviour and pressure drop during the flow of viscoelastic fluids through a microporous medium. The present numerical study aims to investigate and hypothesise about the physical mechanisms governing these complex flow behaviours in an ordered microporous medium consisting of cylindrical micropillars arranged in a staggered configuration. We propose that birefringent strands of high elastic stress, generated by the stretching and alignment of polymer molecules within the porous structure, play the dominant role in controlling the non-monotonic variations in chaos and pressure drop. At low Weissenberg numbers, these stress strands develop gradually, mainly downstream of the micropillars, whereas beyond a critical Weissenberg number, they begin to fluctuate strongly, leading to chaotic flow dynamics. However, at even higher Weissenberg numbers, the strands become larger and stronger, eventually interconnecting between neighbouring micropillars, causing the flow to reorganise into a nearly steady, ordered state similar to that observed at low Weissenberg numbers. On the other hand, the pressure drop in the system consists of a mean contribution, obtained from statistically stationary flow quantities, and a fluctuating contribution. While the mean component increases monotonically with the Weissenberg number, the fluctuating component varies non-monotonically, leading to a similar trend in the total pressure drop. Moreover, the non-monotonic chaotic behaviour strongly depends on the solid volume fraction of the porous medium, which alters both the critical Weissenberg number for instability onset and the range over which the non-monotonic behaviour persists.

</details>


### [86] [Non-normal weakly nonlinear analysis: asymptotic consistency and non-universality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23059)
*Matthew McCormack,Gregory P. Chini,Rich R. Kerswell*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文针对线性稳定但非正态的系统在简谐强迫下的弱非线性行为，发展了一套渐近一致的弱非线性理论，通过定义形式小参数ε为系统最大线性放大的倒数，推导出非普适的振幅方程，能够捕捉非正态性与非线性相互作用诱发的多模态、多频率响应，以及亚临界转换和分岔行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非正态系统中，线性稳定系统仍可能因非正态性产生较大瞬态增长，进而触发非线性转变。理解非正态性与非线性之间的相互作用机制是预测这类系统转捩的关键，但缺乏系统的理论框架。

Method: 定义形式小参数ε为系统最大线性放大的倒数，建立渐近一致的弱非线性框架；在简谐强迫下对通道流及模仿Orr-Sommerfeld-Squire方程结构的二维模型进行分析，推导非普适振幅方程。

Result: 与经典分岔点附近的弱非线性分析不同，所得振幅方程具有非普适性；单一线性模态的放大可通过非线性激发多模态、多频率的前导阶响应。随着强迫幅值增大，约化方程能够捕捉从层流中涌现的稳定非线性状态、后续分岔及其与吸引域边界的碰撞。

Conclusion: 该方法能够描述由强迫和不同初始条件驱动的亚临界转捩，识别超出临界参数后不再存在稳定弱非线性状态的边界，为线性稳定但非正态系统的非线性转捩预测提供了系统性的理论工具。

Abstract: Non-normality can induce large transient growth in linearly stable systems. Determining whether this growth triggers a transition in the underlying nonlinear system, however, requires understanding the interaction between non-normality and nonlinearity. Here, we develop a weakly nonlinear theory for linearly-stable, non-normal systems subject to harmonic forcing, enabling a systematic analysis of this interaction. Following Ducimetière et al. (J. Fluid Mech., vol. 947, 2022, A43), we define a formal small parameter $\varepsilon$ as the reciprocal of the system's maximum linear amplification. However, we ensure asymptotic consistency by providing a framework that naturally adapts to the underlying structure of the system. The approach is applied to a harmonically forced channel flow and to a two-dimensional model mimicking the structure of the Orr-Sommerfeld-Squire equations. Unlike classical weakly nonlinear analysis near bifurcation points, the resulting amplitude equations are non-universal. In fact, a single linear mode amplified by the non-normality can nonlinearly excite a multi-modal and multi-frequency response at leading-order, which is system- or even regime-specific. Nevertheless, the method yields asymptotically consistent amplitude equations that capture this complexity provided a limit in which $\varepsilon\rightarrow0$ can be identified. As the forcing amplitude increases, the reduced equations capture stable nonlinear states emerging from the laminar flow, their subsequent bifurcations, and their eventual collision with the boundary of their basin of attraction. Thus, the amplitude equations can capture subcritical transitions driven by forcing and varied initial conditions and enable the identification of critical parameters beyond which no stable weakly nonlinear state exists.

</details>


### [87] [Scaling of the minimal energy for turbulence transition in pipe flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23269)
*Patrick Keuchel,Daniel Morón,Marc Avila*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过变分方法计算了管流中层流向湍流转换的非线性最优扰动，发现扰动厚度随雷诺数增加按 $Re^{-1/3}$ 标度，转换所需最小扰动能量按 $\mathcal{O}(Re^{-3})$ 标度，并揭示了Orr机制、斜波非线性相互作用和抬升相位的能量增长规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 管流中湍流转捩的预测是流体力学中的一个基本问题，本文旨在通过变分方法计算非线性最优扰动，揭示转捩的能量阈值及其随雷诺数的标度律。

Method: 采用变分方法计算雷诺数 $Re \leq 5000$ 下管流中层流到湍流转换的非线性最优扰动。

Result: 最优扰动结构相似但随 $Re$ 增加而局域化，厚度标度为 $\delta_r \propto Re^{-1/3}$；Orr 阶段能量增益与 $Re$ 线性相关且与初始能量 $E_0$ 无关；斜波和抬升阶段的能量增益受非线性支配，标度为 $\propto Re^2$；转捩所需最小扰动能量标度为 $\mathcal{O}(Re^{-3})$。

Conclusion: 无论雷诺数如何，只要扰动能量超过恒定阈值就会发生转捩；管流中转捩的最小扰动能量随 $Re^{-3}$ 标度衰减。

Abstract: Predicting the transition of turbulence in pipe flow remains a fundamental problem in fluid dynamics. We use a variational approach to compute nonlinear optimal perturbations to the laminar flow at Reynolds number $Re\leq 5000$. As $Re$ increases, optimal perturbations remain structurally similar, but increasingly localize while their thickness scales as $δ_r \propto Re^{-1/3}$. They grow via the Orr mechanism, followed by a phase of strong nonlinear interaction of oblique waves and a lift-up phase. The energy gain during the Orr phase increases linearly with $Re$ and is independent of the initial perturbation energy, $E_0$. The energy gain during the oblique and lift-up phases is governed by nonlinearities and scales as $\propto Re^2$. We find that regardless of the Reynolds number, transition occurs if the energy of the perturbation exceeds a constant threshold. As a result, the minimum perturbation energy required to cause transition in pipe flow scales as $\mathcal{O}(Re^{-3})$.

</details>


### [88] [Data assimilation of flow MRI data into RANS models with algebraic closures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23287)
*C. Namuroy,M. P. Juniper,P. Nair,M. Loecher,D. B. Ennis,A. Marsden,A. Kontogiannis*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 采用贝叶斯推断框架解决逆RANS问题，通过含噪3D Flow MRI数据估计湍流模型参数和入口边界条件的后验概率分布，证明该方法能重建平均流速且不发生过拟合。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RANS模型参数固定，难以适应复杂流动；需要一种方法从实验数据中推断模型参数及其不确定性，以提高湍流模拟的可靠性。

Result: 推断的湍流模型能够在不发生过拟合的情况下重建测量的平均流速，并提供了模型参数的不确定性估计。

Conclusion: 该方法可扩展到更复杂的、可微的RANS模型，为湍流模拟中的参数标定和不确定性量化提供了有效途径。

Abstract: We adopt the Bayesian inference framework to solve an inverse Reynolds-Averaged Navier-Stokes (RANS) problem for the approximate posterior probability distribution of the turbulence model parameters and inlet boundary conditions of a confined turbulent jet. The data are noisy 3D flow MRI measurements of a Newtonian fluid flowing through the Food and Drug Administration (FDA) nozzle geometry. We assimilate this into RANS using two algebraic turbulence models based on the mean shear rate magnitude and the turbulence kinetic energy. We demonstrate that the inferred models are able to reconstruct the measured mean flow velocities without overfitting and we provide uncertainty estimates for the model parameters. The methodology can readily be extended to more complex RANS models, provided that they remain differentiable.

</details>


### [89] [Eulerian Lagrangian relations in decaying two dimensional incompressible Navier Stokes fluids across initial vorticity packing and Reynolds number](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23338)
*Snehanshu Maiti,Shishir Biswas,Rajaraman Ganesh*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文研究了在二维不可压缩Navier-Stokes流体中，涡度填充分数(VPF)和雷诺数(Re)对欧拉-拉格朗日关系的耦合影响。发现在非平衡逆级联 regime 中，欧拉-拉格朗日关系基本保持，输运主要由VPF控制；而在晚期相干涡准平衡 regime 中，欧拉统计平衡对Re不敏感，但示踪粒子输运表现出强烈的Re依赖性，导致直接欧拉-拉格朗日对应关系在宽参数空间内不能普遍维持。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在固定高Re下发现的涡度填充分数(VPF)对欧拉-拉格朗日关系的调控作用，是否能在更宽的(VPF, Re)参数空间中普遍成立。

Method: 在二维不可压缩Navier-Stokes流体的衰减湍流中，系统扫描(VPF, Re)参数空间，分别分析非平衡逆级联 regime 和晚期相干涡准平衡 regime 中欧拉流场演化和拉格朗日示踪粒子输运的耦合行为。

Result: 非平衡逆级联 regime中欧拉-拉格朗日关系基本保持，输运随VPF增加而增强且主要受VPF控制；晚期准平衡 regime中欧拉统计对Re不敏感，但示踪粒子输运呈现强烈的Re依赖的振荡和非单调变化，削弱了VPF排序的输运层级结构。

Conclusion: 固定高Re下发现的直接欧拉-拉格朗日对应关系在更宽的(VPF, Re)参数空间中不能普遍维持。

Abstract: Recent studies Vorticity packing effects on long time turbulent transport in decaying two dimensional incompressible Navier Stokes fluids, Phys. Fluids 38, 045159 (2026) demonstrated that, at a fixed high Reynolds number (Re), the initial vorticity packing fraction (VPF) governs the coupled Eulerian flow evolution and Lagrangian tracer particle transport in decaying two dimensional incompressible Navier Stokes fluids, revealing a strong Eulerian Lagrangian relationship during the nonequilibrium inverse cascade regime and a direct Eulerian Lagrangian correspondence in the late time coherent vortex quasi equilibrium regime, wherein increasing VPF drives transitions from point vortex to patch vortex equilibria and from subdiffusive to superdiffusive transport. In the present work, we investigate how these Eulerian Lagrangian connections evolve across a broad (VPF, Re) parameter space. The results show that the Eulerian Lagrangian relationship remains largely preserved during the nonequilibrium inverse-cascade regime, where transport increases systematically with VPF and remains primarily controlled by VPF despite secondary Re dependent oscillatory modulation. In contrast, the late-time coherent-vortex quasi-equilibrium regime exhibits Eulerian statistical equilibria that remain largely insensitive to Re, while the corresponding tracer-particle transport displays a strong Re dependence characterized by strong oscillatory and nonmonotonic variations across the (VPF, Re) parameter space, substantially weakening the VPF-ordered transport hierarchy observed in the inverse-cascade regime. Consequently, for the parameter range, spatial resolutions, and integration times explored in the present study, the direct Eulerian Lagrangian correspondence identified at fixed high Re is not universally maintained across the broader (VPF, Re) parameter space.

</details>


### [90] [A Methodology to Quantify Interscale Energy Transfer at Solid Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23342)
*Lennard Miller*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合粗粒化与体积惩罚的新型滤波方法，用于量化有界地球物理流体流动（如罗斯贝波反射和海洋边界层形成）中的线性能量传递，弥补了传统傅里叶诊断或滤波方法无法表征线性能量传递的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统傅里叶诊断和滤波方法虽能量化非线性自平流引起的跨尺度能量传递，但无法刻画固体边界附近（如海洋边界层形成、罗斯贝波反射）可能发生的线性能量传递，亟需一种能够物理表征线性能量传递的新方法。

Method: 将粗粒化（coarse-graining）与体积惩罚（volume-penalization）相结合，构建一种新型滤波方法，用于量化有界流动中的线性能量传递。

Result: 通过量化罗斯贝波在西部边界反射时发生的向下尺度能量传递，验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该概念框架具有广泛适用性，可用于研究有界地球物理流体流动的多尺度能量学问题。

Abstract: Far away from solid boundaries, energy can be transferred between different flow scales due to the non-linear self-advection of velocity. This energy transfer can be quantified using well-established Fourier diagnostics or filtering methods. However, these diagnostic tools fail to provide a physical representation of the linear energy transfer that may occur during the formation of oceanic boundary layers or Rossby wave reflections. In this document, I outline a novel filtering methodology that is able to quantify this linear energy transfer by combining coarse-graining with volume-penalization. Its utility is illustrated by quantifying the down-scale energy transfer occuring during a Rossby wave reflection off a western boundary. The conceptual framework developed here is thought to be broadly applicable to the study of multi-scale energetics of bounded geophysical fluid flows.

</details>


### [91] [Continuity equations in the Generalised Lagrangian Mean theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23481)
*Vladimir A. Vladimirov*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文基于广义拉格朗日平均（GLM）理论，系统推导了连续性方程（CEs）在平均流与扰动联合演化中的有效形式，澄清了其有效条件，并推广了McIntyre-Andrews变换（MAT），使其适用于任意流体运动。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 1. 给出了div u与div' u'之间的精确关系式；2. 提出了三种精确CEs形式，并证明了各自的数学不完全性；3. 通过引入相容性方程恢复了CEs的完备性；4. 推广了MAT，使其不再局限于特殊初始条件和辅助PDE，适用于任意流体运动；5. 在小扰动情形下，将结果与经典GLM理论联系起来。

Conclusion: 本文从基础流体动力学原理出发，通过严格的数学推导澄清了GLM框架下连续性方程的有效性条件，并提供了更一般的MAT形式，为平均流-扰动相互作用的建模提供了更坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: Generalised Lagrangian Mean (or Hybrid Euler-Lagrange) theory aims to describe the joint evolution of the mean flow and its perturbations. This paper considers related forms of continuity equations (CEs) and clarifies the conditions of their validity. We do not consider equations of motion; therefore, we use only exact formulae and general notions of fluid dynamics and operate only with the most general statements for CEs. The tools used are Lagrangian X, Eulerian x, averaged Eulerian x' coordinates of fluid particles, and ensemble-based averaging. The targeted forms of CEs are expressed in terms of function x(x',t). Our first step is to present the actual velocity divergence div u via div'u', where u and u' are the actual and average fluid velocities. Then we introduce three versions of exact CEs and demonstrate that each is mathematically incomplete. The third step is to restore their completeness by introducing the compatibility equations required for their validity in general fluid flows. As a basic reference, we present two versions of the McIntyre-Andrews Transformation (MAT) for CEs. The original MAT introduces an auxiliary function that satisfies an auxiliary PDE and special initial conditions. Therefore, it works for a special class of fluid flows. The presented generalisation of MAT makes CEs applicable to arbitrary fluid motion. Both versions require compatibility equations, which we compare with ours. Finally, we consider average flows with small perturbations, thereby linking our exposition to the classical GLM theory.

</details>


### [92] [Scaling patch analysis of turbulent kinetic energy budget equation in wall-bounded flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23520)
*Tie Wei,Zhaorui Li,Sergio Pirozzoli*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文利用标度补丁方法分析壁面湍流中湍动能（TKE）收支方程的平衡结构，将TKE方程划分为近壁粘性底层、内层、外层及中间区等多个标度区域，每个区域具有主控平衡关系和相应标度参数，并引入介尺度标度实现内外层平滑过渡，最终形成统一的解释框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有壁面湍流TKE收支方程在不同区域（近壁区、内层、外层）呈现出不同的主导平衡和标度行为，缺乏统一的理论框架来解释其结构特征和雷诺数依赖性。作者旨在通过标度补丁方法，系统划分各区域的标度参数和主导平衡关系，形成自洽的、统一的TKE收支结构描述。

Method: 采用标度补丁方法，将TKE收支方程按空间区域划分：1) 近壁粘性底层（粘性扩散与耗散主导，尺度由运动粘度和壁面耗散率决定，厚度约Kolmogorov尺度）；2) 内层（以TKE产生峰值为参考尺度，得到传统内标度，并将粘性扩散与耗散项合并以增强不同雷诺数下的坍塌）；3) 外层（利用Prandtl混合长模型导出TKE产生的特征尺度）；4) 中间区（引入介尺度标度，确保内外层平滑过渡）。

Result: 明确了各区域的标度参数和主控平衡：粘性底层以Kolmogorov尺度为特征长度；内层以产生峰值为特征尺度，合并粘性扩散与耗散能改善雷诺数坍塌；外层可由混合长模型导出生产项标度；介尺度标度成功连接内外层。标度补丁框架统一描述了壁面湍流TKE收支的整体结构。

Conclusion: 标度补丁方法为壁面湍流TKE收支方程提供了一个统一、自洽的标度框架，能够涵盖从近壁区到外层的所有区域，并解释不同区域的主导平衡关系及其雷诺数依赖性。

Abstract: The scaling patch approach is applied to analyze the turbulent kinetic energy (TKE) budget equation in wall-bounded turbulent flows. The balance of the TKE equation is divided into several distinct regions, or scaling patches, each characterized by a dominant balance among the governing terms and its own appropriate scaling parameters. In the near-wall viscous sublayer, the TKE balance is primarily between viscous diffusion and dissipation, and the characteristic scales are set by the kinematic viscosity and the wall dissipation rate. The thickness of this sublayer is on the order of the Kolmogorov length scale. Moving away from the wall, the peak TKE production provides a natural reference scale for the inner layer, yielding the traditional inner scaling. Grouping the viscous diffusion and dissipation terms in the inner layer enhances the collapse across different Reynolds numbers. In the outer region, Prandtl's mixing-length model is used to derive a characteristic scale for TKE production. A new meso-scaling is further introduced to describe the intermediate region, ensuring a smooth transition between the inner and outer layers. The scaling patch framework offers a unified interpretation of the structure and scaling behavior of the TKE budget across all regions of wall-bounded turbulence.

</details>

### [93] [Absolute Radar-REMPI via Two-Color REMPI and Absolutely Calibrated CMS for Diagnostics of Species in Gaseous Mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20827)
*Mathis Malaussena,Liam West,Kenneth Reindersma,Nicholas Babusis,Alexey Shashurin*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了绝对雷达-REMPI诊断技术的初步开发，该技术通过双色共振增强多光子电离（REMPI）和绝对校准的相干微波散射（CMS），实现了对任意气态物种的绝对校准测量。使用355 nm和241-242 nm双激光束对分子氧（O₂）进行了双色REMPI实验，ICCD相机验证了在光束交汇区域成功产生了与O₂目标跃迁相关的可测电离信号，为高灵敏度、实时诊断高超声速激波风洞和电推进系统等非稳态流场中的绝对物种密度奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Radar-REMPI技术虽然能高灵敏度检测气态物种，但缺乏绝对校准能力，无法直接测量物种的绝对密度。作者旨在开发一种通用的、可绝对校准的诊断方法，以应用于高超声速激波风洞和电推进系统等非稳态流场中的实时诊断。

Method: 采用双色共振增强多光子电离（REMPI）与绝对校准的相干微波散射（CMS）相结合。使用355 nm和241-242 nm可调谐参量光束两束交叉激光对分子氧（O₂）进行双色REMPI实验，通过ICCD相机验证电离信号。

Result: 双光束配置成功在光束交汇区域产生了与O₂目标跃迁相关的可测量电离信号，ICCD相机成像验证了该结果。

Conclusion: 该研究为Absolute Radar-REMPI技术的进一步发展奠定了坚实基础，该技术有望实现对高超声速激波风洞和电推进系统等非稳态流场中绝对物种密度的高灵敏度、实时诊断。

Abstract: This work demonstrates the initial development of the Absolute Radar-REMPI diagnostic technique, a development of the Radar-REMPI technique that provides an approach for absolutely calibrated measurements, which is universally applicable to any type of tested gaseous species. Absolute measurements are achieved through the utilization of two-color Resonance Enhanced Multiphoton Ionization (REMPI) and absolutely calibrated Coherent Microwave Scattering (CMS). Two-color REMPI of molecular oxygen (O2) was demonstrated using two intersected laser beams: a 355 nm beam and a tunable parametric beam at 241-242 nm. The results confirm that the two-beam configuration can successfully generate measurable ionization associated with the targeted transition of O2 within the beam intersection region, as verified using an ICCD camera. These findings establish a foundation for the further development of Absolute Radar-REMPI, which has the potential to enable highly sensitive, real-time diagnostics of absolute species densities in unsteady flowfields such as those occurring in hypersonic shock tunnels and electric propulsion systems.

### [94] [Phase-Space Energy Transfer of Wave-Particle Interactions using the Field-Particle Correlation Technique and Linear Plasma Theory with JET-PLUME](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20865)
*Collin R. Brown,Gregory G. Howes,Kristopher G. Klein,Jason M. TenBarge*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出JET-PLUME方法，基于解析傅里叶空间的场-粒子相关技术，系统分析等离子体中波-粒子相互作用的相空间能量传递，揭示了动力学阿尔芬波阻尼过程中垂直电场驱动平行离子流的新物理机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的场-粒子相关（FPC）技术虽能揭示粒子加速的速度空间特征，但完全映射这些特征需要大量动理学模拟或观测数据，成本极高，亟需一种高效的系统性分析方法。

Method: 在PLUME Vlasov-Maxwell色散求解器基础上扩展得到JET-PLUME，利用其对平行漂移双麦克斯韦分布建模的能力，加入解析傅里叶空间的FPC公式，实现对各共振贡献的隔离、简并熵模分量的分离以及不稳定增长模式的系统分析。

Result: 推导了无量纲表达式以推广结果适用范围；发现动力学阿尔芬波阻尼过程中，垂直电场能驱动大垂直速度粒子的平行离子流，从而降低净朗道阻尼；展示了通过JET-PLUME获得的速度空间特征。

Conclusion: 解析形式的相空间能量传递公式能够揭示不同等离子体环境中波-粒子相互作用的新物理，JET-PLUME为系统研究这一过程提供了有效工具。

Abstract: The collisionless transfer of energy between fields and particles through wave-particle interactions is a fundamental process in space plasmas but remains incompletely characterized because many mechanisms operate across a wide parameter range and diverse plasma conditions. The Field-Particle Correlation (FPC) technique reveals velocity-space signatures of particle energization by correlating measured electric field fluctuations with changes in the velocity distribution. Fully mapping these signatures across plasma parameters requires an impractically large number of kinetic simulations or observations. To address this challenge, we introduce JET-PLUME (Judging Energy Transfer in a Plasma in a Linear Uniform Magnetized Environment), an extension of the PLUME Vlasov-Maxwell dispersion solver. JET-PLUME uses PLUME's ability to model parallel drifting bi-Maxwellian distributions to examine phase-space energy transfer by adding an analytic Fourier-space formulation of the FPC. This approach isolates the contribution of individual resonances, separates degenerate entropy mode components, and allows systematic analysis of unstable, growing modes. Dimensionless expressions extend the results across a broad parameter range and highlight the role of off-diagonal elements of the susceptibility tensor in coupling electric field and current response. We show that during kinetic Alfvén wave damping, the perpendicular field can drive parallel ion currents among particles with large perpendicular velocity, reducing the net Landau damping. The resulting velocity-space signatures, accessible through JET-PLUME, demonstrate how analytic formulations of phase-space energy transfer can reveal novel physics of wave-particle interactions across diverse plasma environments.

</details>

*Seungjun Lee,Woojin Nam,Gunsu Yun*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等离子体-液体界面处的界面动力学对传质过程至关重要，但表面不稳定性与界面传输之间的机制关系尚不清楚。

Result: 钠传输通过液滴发射实现，经历表面形变、Taylor锥形成和电喷雾三个阶段；Na I光学发射的阈值电压随表面张力降低而减小，与线性扰动分析预测的电液动力学不稳定边界条件吻合。

Conclusion: 电液动力学驱动的液滴发射是钠离子从液体进入等离子体的主要机制，表面不稳定性是溶解物种从液体注入等离子体的活性传输通道，构建了独特的等离子体化学反应网络。

Abstract: Interfacial dynamics play a central role in transport processes across the plasma-liquid interface. While the strong electric field in the plasma sheath can destabilize the liquid surface and induce species transfer from the liquid into the plasma, the mechanistic relationship between surface instability and interfacial transport remains poorly understood. Here, we investigate the transport of sodium species in an atmospheric-pressure helium plasma in contact with a negatively DC-biased NaCl electrolyte using high-speed imaging, laser Mie scattering, and optical emission spectroscopy. The results show that Na transport is mediated by droplet emission from the liquid surface and proceeds through three sequential stages: surface deformation, Taylor cone formation, and electrospray. The threshold voltage required for Na I optical emission decreases with decreasing surface tension, in good agreement with the marginal condition for electrohydrodynamic (EHD) instability predicted by linear perturbation analysis. These findings demonstrate that EHD-driven droplet emission is the primary mechanism carrying sodium ions from the liquid into the plasma, where they are neutralized and excited. More broadly, this work establishes surface instability as the active transport channel governing the injection of dissolved species from the liquid into the plasma, creating a unique reaction network of plasma chemistry.

</details>


### [96] [Wave-Energy Partition Governs Weak Collisional Damping in Cold Plasmas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21087)
*Yanzeng Zhang,Xianzhu Tang,Ge Zhuang*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 弱碰撞阻尼在冷等离子体中由波能分配决定，阻尼率等于碰撞频率乘以等离子体运动所储存的波能比例，这统一解释了不同分支间阻尼差异的物理起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的介电张量计算难以揭示不同分支间碰撞阻尼差异的物理起源，作者希望建立一个更直观、物理透明的阻尼定律。

Method: 基于单速率冷等离子体模型，将标准微扰阻尼公式重新表述为波能分配形式，并利用解析例子（朗缪尔波、横电磁波、哨声波、异常波）进行验证。

Conclusion: 该能量分配形式统一了不同分支的弱碰撞阻尼分类，为多分支等离子体波系统中的模式竞争提供了简单诊断工具。

Abstract: Weak dissipation can control wave propagation, mode competition, and instability thresholds in plasmas, yet the physical origin of large branch-to-branch differences in collisional damping is often obscured by dielectric-tensor calculations. We show that weak collisional damping in cold plasmas is governed by wave-energy partition. In the one-rate cold-plasma model, the damping rate of a collisionless eigenmode is exactly the collision frequency multiplied by the fraction of the total wave energy stored in plasma motion. This result recasts the standard perturbative damping formula into a compact and physically transparent law, immediately explaining why field-dominated branches such as whistlers can be much less damped than the collision frequency, whereas quasi-electrostatic modes can exhibit damping of comparable magnitude. Analytic examples for Langmuir, transverse electromagnetic, whistler, and extraordinary waves show that the energy-partition form classifies weak collisional damping across distinct branches and provides a simple diagnostic for mode competition in multibranch plasma-wave systems.

</details>


### [97] [Test Particle Study of EDI Driven Electron Transport in a Hall Thruster Using PIC Derived Electric Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21143)
