Abstract: Electron transport in a Hall thruster is investigated at the test particle level using prescribed electric fields derived from the electron drift instability (EDI) resolving three dimensional particle-in-cell (PIC) simulation. Four primary electric field configurations are considered: static averaged field, full PIC field, proper orthogonal decomposition (POD) reconstructed field, and a simplified analytical Ey field. Two additional control cases, the magnetic-field-only case and the denoised PIC field, are also included. Transport statistics show that the averaged field produces only weak axial cross field transport. In contrast, the full PIC field produces clear fluctuation driven transport, characterized by pronounced negative axial displacement, enhanced channel entry, finite channel residence, particle energization, and appreciable anode directed loss. POD analysis shows that the transport relevant EDI electric field structures are distributed over multiple coupled modes, and that a moderate truncation order of approximately 20 modes is required to recover the main transport signatures. The analytical Ey model separately examines the influence of azimuthal electric field fluctuations on axial electron transport and shows that fluctuation amplitude is a primary determinant.

</details>


### [98] [Rankine-Hugoniot conditions in Q-variables: a wave-aligned formulation of MHD discontinuities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21193)
*Anna Krupka,Tijs Van Hoof,Tom Van Doorsselaere*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了Q-变量形式的MHD Rankine-Hugoniot跳跃条件，证明其与经典条件等价，为波-激波相互作用分析提供了波对齐框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Q-变量形式在描述MHD波动和湍流方面已被证明有效，但其在等离子体间断（如激波）中的行为尚未被建立，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 通过将理想MHD方程重写为适合激波系参考系跳跃分析的形式，推导了质量、动量、磁通量和能量的跳跃关系，并解析证明了其与经典Rankine-Hugoniot条件的等价性。

Result: 得到了Q-变量形式的完整Rankine-Hugoniot跳跃条件集合，并证明了这些关系与经典MHD Rankine-Hugoniot条件精确等价。

Conclusion: 该形式为MHD间断提供了波对齐表示，当波支参数α与相关特征速度一致时，可自然讨论方向波内容和分支限制极限，适用于太阳风、磁层系统及UAWSOM等结构化等离子体环境中的波-激波相互作用分析。

Abstract: The recently developed Q-variable formalism generalises the Elsässer representation by providing a wave-aligned representation applicable to a broad class of magnetohydrodynamic disturbances, including Alfvénic, fast, slow, and kink waves. While this framework has proven useful for the study of wave dynamics and turbulence, its behaviour in the presence of plasma discontinuities has not yet been established. In this work, we derive the complete set of Rankine-Hugoniot jump conditions in terms of the Q-variables by rewriting the ideal MHD equations in a form suitable for shock-frame jump analysis. This yields explicit jump relations for mass, momentum, magnetic flux, and energy. We then demonstrate analytically that these relations are exactly equivalent to the classical MHD Rankine-Hugoniot conditions. This reformulation provides a wave-aligned representation of MHD discontinuities and offers a natural framework for discussing directional wave content and branch-restricted limits when $α$, the wave-branch parameter entering the Q-variable definition, is chosen consistently with the relevant characteristic speed. The resulting formulation is well suited for the analysis of wave-shock interactions in magnetised plasmas, with potential applications to the solar wind, magnetospheric systems, and large-scale models of structured plasma environments such as UAWSOM.

</details>


### [99] [Effect of Noise on Spatio-Temporal Evolution of Current Filamentation Instability in Relativistic Beam-Plasma Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21221)
*Thulasidharan K,Vishwa Bandhu Pathak*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过解析和二维PIC模拟研究相对论束-等离子体系统中电流丝化不稳定性（CFI）的时空演化，推导了包含二阶空间导数项的横向矢势偏微分方程，发现该导数项对束前沿附近的空间增长起主导作用。对于恒定初始噪声，数值解能重现模拟中的磁场结构，而忽略该项的解析解则不能。饱和长度满足$L_{\mathrm{sat}}\propto(v_{0b}+2)v_{0b}/γ_{0b}^{3}$，且随时间线性增加，速率$\mathrm{d}L_{\mathrm{sat}}/\mathrm{d}τ\approx0.42\,c$。当束速度超过$0.6c$时，斜模和非线性丝动力学导致模型偏离模拟结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解和准确描述相对论电子束注入冷等离子体时电流丝化不稳定性（CFI）的时空演化过程，特别是束前沿附近的空间增长行为以及纵向场调制的物理起源。

Method: 采用解析推导建立横向矢势的偏微分方程（包含二阶空间导数项），结合二维粒子云（PIC）模拟进行验证。分别对恒定、线性增长和振荡初始噪声情况进行解析（忽略二阶项时）和数值求解（包含二阶项时）。

Result: 1）二阶空间导数项对束前沿附近的空间增长至关重要，忽略该项无法重现模拟磁场结构；2）纵向场调制是CFI的固有属性，即使恒定噪声也会产生；3）饱和长度$L_{\mathrm{sat}}\propto(v_{0b}+2)v_{0b}/γ_{0b}^{3}$，随时间线性增长速率为$0.42\,c$；4）束速度$>0.6c$时，斜模和非线性效应导致模型失效。

Conclusion: 二阶空间导数项改变了不稳定性从束前沿向后的空间输运过程，而非局域增长率本身；噪声分布可影响时空演化；模型适用性受限于束速度较低且忽略斜模和非线性丝动力学的单模处理范围。

Abstract: The spatio-temporal evolution of the current filamentation instability in a relativistic beam--plasma system is studied analytically and with two-dimensional particle-in-cell simulations. A partial differential equation for the transverse vector potential is derived for a sharp-front relativistic beam entering cold, unmagnetized plasma, including a second-order spatial derivative term that governs the spatial growth near the beam front. The equation is solved analytically for constant, linearly growing, and oscillatory initial noise when this term is neglected, and numerically when it is included, as no closed-form solution then exists. For constant initial noise, the numerical solution reproduces the simulated magnetic-field structure, unlike the analytical solution without the term. This shows that the longitudinal field modulation is intrinsic to the instability, present even for a noise with constant amplitudes. The noise profile as well can influence the spatial-temporal evolution of the instability, which we discuss further considering linearly growing and oscillatory noise. The field grows spatially behind the beam front and saturates at a length $L_{\mathrm{sat}}\propto(v_{0b}+2)v_{0b}/γ_{0b}^{3}$, where $v_{0b}$ and $γ_{0b}$ are the beam velocity and Lorentz factor, beyond which growth is purely temporal. The saturation length increases linearly in time at a constant rate $\mathrm{d}L_{\mathrm{sat}}/\mathrm{d}τ\approx0.42\,c$, matching the analytical estimate. The temporal growth rate remains unchanged, so the term modifies the spatial transport of the instability rather than its local amplification. For beam velocities above $0.6c$, the model deviates from the simulations as oblique modes and nonlinear filament dynamics outside the single-mode treatment become important.

</details>


### [100] [Semi-local Floquet theory for active azimuthal magnetic modulation of Hall-thruster high-frequency instabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21265)
*Yinjian Zhao,Changzheng Hu*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 本文发展了半局域Floquet扩展的均匀场动力学电子漂移不稳定性（EDI）色散关系，用于评估霍尔推进器中预设方位角磁场调制作为高频不稳定性线性预筛选工具的效果。研究发现，正弦方位角磁调制虽然能展宽耦合谱并重新分配不稳定增长，但无法实现稳健的线性稳定化，应被解释为频谱再分布机制而非线性稳定化机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估霍尔推进器中预设方位角磁场调制作为高频不稳定性线性预筛选工具的可行性。

Method: 发展半局域Floquet扩展的均匀场EDI色散关系，将均匀动力学响应作为局域谱核，通过正弦磁调制耦合Floquet边带，将标量色散条件替换为有限矩阵色散问题。数值过程结合标量均匀场预测器、行列式修正、奇异值诊断、边带权重分析和截断检查。

Result: 正弦方位角磁调制展宽了耦合谱，在不稳定增长在低波数修正双流状和回旋共振范围内重新分布。某些长波长、中大幅度案例减少了综合正增长度量，但未实现峰值增长包络的稳健抑制。无测试案例产生有限稳定Bloch区间。

Conclusion: 在当前冷离子半局域Floquet模型下，预设方位角磁调制应被解释为频谱再分布机制，而非稳健的线性稳定化机制。

Abstract: A semi-local Floquet extension of a uniform-field kinetic electron drift instability (EDI) dispersion relation is developed to assess prescribed azimuthal magnetic-field modulation as a linear pre-screening tool for Hallthruster high-frequency instabilities. The uniform kinetic response is used as a local spectral kernel, while a sinusoidal magnetic modulation couples Floquet sidebands and replaces the scalar dispersion condition by a finite matrix dispersion problem. The numerical procedure combines scalar uniform-field predictors, determinant correction, singular-value diagnostics, sideband-weight analysis, and truncation checks. Because a single Floquet root contains multiple physical wave numbers, stability is assessed with the upper growth envelope over the Bloch zone rather than with an individual projected azimuthal wave-number branch. Parameter scans over modulation wavelength and amplitude show that sinusoidal azimuthal magnetic modulation broadens the coupled spectrum and redistributes unstable growth among low-wave-number modified-two-stream-like and cyclotron-resonant ranges. Some long-wavelength, moderate-to-large-amplitude cases reduce integrated positive growth measures, but these reductions are not accompanied by robust suppression of the peak growth envelope. No tested case produces a finite stable Bloch interval. Within the present cold-ion semi-local Floquet model, prescribed azimuthal magnetic modulation is therefore better interpreted as a spectral-redistribution mechanism than as a robust linear stabilization mechanism by itself.

</details>


### [101] [Reconnection-induced electron energization in magnetospheric Kelvin-Helmholtz dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21293)
*Silvia Ferro,Fabio Bacchini,Giuseppe Arrò,Francesco Pucci,Pierre Henri*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过二维全动力学模拟研究了无碰撞Kelvin-Helmholtz不稳定性(KHI)中电子加热与磁重联动力学之间的直接联系，发现非线性阶段碎片化电流片中的重联活动导致各向异性(T∥,e > T⊥,e)的非热电子加速，离子作为主要能量库向电磁场传递能量，电子获得净正能量输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有动理学研究显示非线性KHI演化中存在高效电子加热，但重联动力学与局域电子能量化之间的关联仍未被充分理解，本文旨在建立这一直接联系。

Method: 采用二维全动力学模拟，在具有两个速度剪切层和均匀引导场的双周期系统中研究KHI演化，系统初始化为有限拉莫尔半径平衡态。

Result: 发现KHI非线性阶段中，初始相干涡旋演化为由碎片化电流片构成的层状结构并伴随重联活动；离子是主要能量库，向电磁场传递能量，电子获得净正能量输入；电子能量化呈现强各向异性(T∥,e > T⊥,e)，局域于间歇性电流片内，并伴随超热尾分布。

Conclusion: 建立了无碰撞KHI动力学中重联相关电流结构与局域电子能量化之间的直接联系，且两个不同涡度取向的剪切层表现出相似的统计行为。

Abstract: The Kelvin-Helmholtz instability (KHI) is a major driver of multiscale plasma dynamics at velocity shear layers, where it can promote the formation of current sheets and the onset of magnetic reconnection as well as drive plasma energization. While recent kinetic studies have shown efficient electron heating during nonlinear KH evolution, the connection between reconnection dynamics and localized electron energization is still not fully understood. We investigate this link using two-dimensional fully kinetic simulations of KHI developing in a double-periodic system with two velocity shear layers and a uniform guide field, initialized from a finite-Larmor-radius equilibrium. During the nonlinear stage, initially coherent vortices evolve into layers populated by fragmented current sheets displaying reconnection activity. The global energetics reveal species-dependent energization pathways. Ions act as the primary energy reservoir, transferring energy to the electromagnetic fields, while electrons receive the dominant net positive energy input. Electron energization is strongly anisotropic ($T_{\parallel,e} > T_{\perp,e}$) and localized within intermittent current sheets associated with enhanced field-particle energy exchange and elevated agyrotropy. These regions also show the development of suprathermal tails in the electron energy distributions, providing evidence for nonthermal electron energization. Despite opposite vorticity orientations, the two shear layers exhibit similar statistical behavior. Together, these results establish a direct connection between reconnection-associated current structures and localized electron energization in collisionless KHI dynamics.

</details>


### [102] [Geometric numerical discretization of electromagnetic quasineutral models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21418)
*Nishant Narechania,Emil Poulsen,Eric Sonnendrucker*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 本文扩展了GEMPICX框架，使用拟线性全动力学Vlasov-Maxwell方程在双重网格上通过拟保有限差分求解，采用离散旋度-旋度方程隐式求解电场，并利用拉格朗日乘子维持电流密度散度在机器精度范围内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将GEMPICX框架从完整Vlasov-Maxwell方程扩展到拟中性条件，解决拟中性模型中电场时间导数不直接出现在动力学系统中的问题。

Method: 基于离散作用量原理，利用双重网格的对偶性；采用离散旋度-旋度方程隐式求解电场（无需电势）；引入拉格朗日乘子约束电流密度散度在机器精度水平。

Result: 实现了拟中性全动力学Vlasov-Maxwell方程在双重网格上的离散求解，电场通过隐式方式获得，电流密度散度被精确维持在机器精度。

Conclusion: 本文成功扩展了GEMPICX框架以处理拟中性等离子体模拟，避免了直接求解电势，并保证了电荷守恒的数值精度。

Abstract: In this work, the geometric electromagnetic Particle-in-Cell (PIC) framework, GEMPICX, is extended to solve the quasineutral, fully kinetic Vlasov-Maxwell equations on dual grids using mimetic finite differences. The discrete action principle is derived, taking into account the duality between the grids. The temporal derivative of the electric field does not directly appear in the dynamical system for the quasineutral model. Hence, a discretized curl-curl equation is used to implicitly obtain the electric field at every time-step. This also circumvents the need to obtain electric potentials. A Lagrange multiplier is used to maintain the discretized divergence of the current density at machine zero.

</details>


### [103] [Geometric numerical discretization of a quasineutral hybrid model of drift-kinetic electrons and fully kinetic ions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21423)
*Nishant Narechania,Guo Meng,Emil Poulsen,Eric Sonnendruecker*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 本文将GEMPICX框架扩展至准中性混合Vlasov-Maxwell方程，采用保结构有限差分双网格方法，通过隐式求解curl-curl方程获得平行电场分量，并测试了全显式和两种IMEX时间推进方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展几何电磁粒子云(PIC)框架GEMPICX，使其能够求解准中性混合Vlasov-Maxwell方程，处理漂移动力学电子和全动力学离子的混合模型。

Method: 采用保结构有限差分双网格方法，推导混合模型的离散作用原理；平行电场分量通过隐含的curl-curl方程求解，垂直电场分量通过无位移电流的准中性安培方程结合漂移动力学模型中的电流定义获得；测试了全显式和两种IMEX时间推进格式。

Result: 离散化的电场方程形成大规模稀疏线性系统；通过色散关系验证了各种波模，确认了数值模型的有效性。

Conclusion: 成功将GEMPICX框架扩展到准中性混合模型，验证了保结构双网格方法在处理此类问题时的有效性。

Abstract: We extend the geometric electromagnetic particle-in-cell (PIC) framework, GEMPICX, to solve the quasineutral hybrid Vlasov-Maxwell equations with drift-kinetic electrons and fully kinetic ions. A structure-preserving finite difference method that employs dual grids is used. The discrete action principle for the hybrid model is derived, using the dual nature of the grids. The dynamical system for this hybrid quasineutral model does not explicitly involve the temporal evolution term for the electric field. A curl-curl equation is therefore used to implicitly obtain the component of the electric field that is parallel to the background magnetic field, at every timestep. The perpendicular component of the electric field is obtained using the quasineutral Ampere's equation without the displacement current, combined with the definition of the current in the drift-kinetic model. The discretized versions of the electric field equations are large, sparse linear systems. A fully explicit time-stepping scheme as well as two implicit-explicit (IMEX) schemes are tested. The numerical model is validated by verifying the various waves obtained from the dispersion relation.

</details>


### [104] [Linear Tearing Growth and Onset of Relativistic Magnetic Reconnection in the Presence of Shear Flows and a Guide Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21505)
*Sarah Peery,Yi-Hsin Liu*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过动力学粒子模拟和数值求解器，研究了剪切流和导向场对相对论性磁重联触发时间的影响，发现两者均会减缓撕裂模不稳定的线性增长率，并在高剪切流下观察到向开尔文-亥姆霍兹不稳定性的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非相对论撕裂理论已表明剪切流会减缓撕裂不稳定性的线性阶段并延迟磁重联触发，本文旨在将这一研究拓展到相对论情形，探究剪切流和导向场强度对相对论性磁重联触发时间的影响。

Method: 采用动力学粒子网格（PIC）模拟方法，并开发了包含运动电场效应的相对论线性撕裂不稳定性增长率数值求解器。

Result: 剪切流和导向场均会减缓撕裂模增长率；在高剪切流条件下，系统会通过一个中间过渡区转变为线性开尔文-亥姆霍兹不稳定性。

Conclusion: 剪切流和导向场都会延迟相对论性磁重联的触发，且高剪切流会导致不稳定性模式的转变。

Abstract: It has been shown in non-relativistic tearing theory that shear flows will slow the linear phase of tearing instability and can delay onset of magnetic reconnection. We find using kinetic particle-in-cell simulations that shear flow as well as guide field strength affect the onset time of relativistic magnetic reconnection. To model this we develop a numerical solver for the growth rate of the relativistic linear tearing instability, including effects of the motional electric field which has not previously been done. We find slowing of growth due to both shear flows and guide field, and at higher flow shear, transition through an intermediate regime to linear Kelvin-Helmholtz instability.

</details>


### [105] [Focused Coherent Microwave Scattering for Spatially Resolved Electron Number Density Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21520)
*H. Jenrow,K. Reindersma,A. Shashurin*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了聚焦相干微波散射（F-CMS）诊断技术的初步验证，通过使用PTFE透镜聚焦探测微波束实现空间分辨测量，在约5×10^9 cm^{-3}电子数密度的辉光放电等离子体中成功检测到信号，为开发高灵敏度、时空分辨的实时电子数密度测量技术奠定基础，预期灵敏度可达约10^8 cm^{-3}，适用于高超声速激波风洞和航天器电推进系统等非稳态流场诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统相干微波散射（CMS）技术缺乏空间分辨能力，无法满足对非稳态流场（如高超声速激波风洞和航天器电推进系统）进行高灵敏度、时空分辨的电子数密度实时测量的需求。

Method: 采用PTFE（聚四氟乙烯）透镜聚焦探测微波束，隔离出特定的探测体积，实现了聚焦相干微波散射（F-CMS）技术，并在电子数密度约5×10^9 cm^{-3}的辉光放电等离子体中开展了可行性验证实验。

Result: 在放电启动时刻成功检测到清晰的F-CMS输出信号，验证了该技术的可行性。预期灵敏度可低至约10^8 cm^{-3}，且无需信号累加即可实现实时测量。

Conclusion: F-CMS技术具备实现高灵敏度、时空分辨的电子数密度实时测量的潜力，无需信号累加，为未来进一步技术开发奠定了基础，将在高超声速激波风洞和航天器电推进系统等非稳态流场诊断中具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: This work provides an initial demonstration of Focused Coherent Microwave Scattering (F-CMS) diagnostics technique, an extension of the conventional Coherent Microwave Scattering (CMS) technique that enables spatially resolved measurements. The spatial-resolution functionality was achieved using PTFE lenses to focus the probing microwave beam and isolate a distinct probing volume. The feasibility of F-CMS was demonstrated using glow-discharge plasma volume with an electron number density of approximately 5x10$^9$ cm$^{-3}$ as a test object. A clear F-CMS output signal was successfully detected upon the discharge initiation. These findings establish a foundation for the further development of the F-CMS technique, which has the potential to enable highly sensitive, spatiotemporally resolved measurements of electron number density in real time, without the need for signal accumulation (sensitivity down to an electron number density of approximately 10$^8$ cm$^{-3}$ is expected). Such capability will be useful for a variety of applications including diagnostics of unsteady flowfields such as those occurring in hypersonic shock tunnels and spacecraft electric propulsion systems.

</details>


### [106] [Plasma Flow Generation and Particle Acceleration from Expanding Magnetic Bubbles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21853)
*Yang Zhang,Brandon K. Russell,Geoffrey Pomraning,Lan Gao,Xiaocan Li,Adam Stainer,William Daughton,Chuanfei Dong,Liang Wang,Peiyun Shi,Kian Orr,Hantao Ji*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 该论文通过动理学模拟和激光驱动电容-线圈实验，发现上升电流会排斥等离子体，形成膨胀的磁泡并加速粒子，其膨胀前沿速度由内边缘磁场和外边缘等离子体密度决定的阿尔芬速度标定。这一机制将脉冲电流驱动确立为实验室等离子体中产生等离子体流和加速粒子的基本方式，并可能与天体物理学相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验室中脉冲等离子体动力学常由上升电流驱动，但其定量等离子体响应尚未明确建立，因此作者旨在阐明这一基本物理过程。

Method: 采用全动理学粒子网格（PIC）模拟和激光驱动电容-线圈实验相结合的方法。

Result: 上升电流会排斥等离子体，形成膨胀磁泡并加速粒子；膨胀前沿速度由内边缘磁场和外边缘等离子体密度决定的阿尔芬速度标定。

Conclusion: 脉冲电流驱动是实验室等离子体产生等离子体流和加速粒子的基本机制，并可能对天体物理学具有潜在意义。

Abstract: Impulsive plasma dynamics in the laboratory are often driven by rising electric currents, yet their quantitative plasma response has not been well established. By means of fully kinetic particle-in-cell simulations and laser-driven capacitor-coil experiments, we show that a rising current expels plasma, forming an expanding magnetic bubble and accelerating particles. The expansion front velocity scales with the Alfvén speed determined by the magnetic field at its inner edge and the plasma density at its outer edge. This mechanism establishes impulsive current drive as a fundamental way that generates plasma flows and accelerates particles in laboratory plasmas, with potential relevance to astrophysics.

</details>


### [107] [Plasma turbulence driven by wave-hole interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22098)
*Gabriele Celebre,Mario Imbrogno,Sergio Servidio,Francesco Valentini*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过高分辨率Vlasov-Poisson模拟，揭示了静电波与德拜及亚德拜尺度密度空隙的相互作用如何在全相空间重新分布强各向异性能量级联，展示了波-障碍绕射向相空间湍流转变的非线性动力学过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解波-障碍绕射作为向非线性、湍流和复杂性转变的范例，特别是在等离子体动力学中该相互作用如何导致相空间湍流。

Method: 采用理想化动理学等离子体模型，通过高分辨率Vlasov-Poisson数值模拟，分析静电波与密度空隙在德拜及亚德拜尺度的相互作用。

Result: 发现波-空隙相互作用在全相空间中重新分布强各向异性能量级联，揭示了不均匀性对结构形成和小尺度定向湍流的影响。

Conclusion: 波-障碍绕射是向非线性过渡的典型范例，本研究将其推广到等离子体相空间湍流，阐明了不均匀性在能量级联和结构形成中的关键作用。

Abstract: Wave-obstacle diffraction is, par excellence, an example of transition to nonlinearity, generating turbulence and complexity in fluids. We present an idealized kinetic plasma regime capturing this ubiquitous interaction and its transition to phase-space turbulence. High-resolution Vlasov-Poisson simulations reveal that the interplay between electrostatic waves and density gaps at Debye and sub-Debye scales redistributes a strongly anisotropic energy cascade throughout the full phase space, unveiling the effect of inhomogeneities on structure formation and small-scale-directed turbulent flow.

</details>


### [108] [Resonant Pitch-Angle Scattering Of Runaway-Electrons by Externally-launched Helicon Waves in the DIII-D Tokamak](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22141)
*Hari Choudhury,Jeffrey Lestz,Carlos Paz-Soldan,Alexander Battey,Nils Leuthold,Andrey Lvovskiy,Claudio Marini,Jayson Barr,William Heidbrink,Donald Spong,Shawn Tang,Bart Van Compernolle,Qile Zhang,Yanzeng Zhang,Xianzhu Tang*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了DIII-D托卡马克中螺旋波（哨声波）与逃逸电子之间的共振波粒相互作用，发现正常多普勒共振（n=1）可有效限制逃逸电子增长，而反常多普勒共振（n=-1）反而增强逃逸电子群体；同时讨论了天线对准、波传播方向及耦合功率的影响，并指出碰撞阻尼和真空间隙是螺旋波用于散射逃逸电子的主要障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在前期工作（Choudhury等, Phys. Rev. Lett. 2026）已验证螺旋波可散射逃逸电子的基础上，进一步探索天线对准、波传播方向及耦合功率等参数对逃逸电子散射效果的影响，为托卡马克中失控电子缓解提供更全面的物理理解。

Method: 在DIII-D托卡马克的静态逃逸电子实验场景中，对比了理想天线对准与非理想对准两种构型；通过改变螺旋波发射方向（对应正常多普勒共振n=1和反常多普勒共振n=-1）及耦合功率，测量逃逸电子群体的增长行为；利用快速磁测量诊断观测螺旋波关闭期间的频率上扫现象（30-60 MHz）。

Result: 理想天线构型下螺旋波防止了逃逸电子增长；非理想构型下存在功率阈值，超过阈值后n=1共振可有效限制逃逸电子增长；n=-1共振反而增强逃逸电子群体；螺旋波关闭期间观察到30-60 MHz频率上扫信号；碰撞阻尼和真空间隙显著阻碍螺旋波进入等离子体芯部。

Conclusion: 正常多普勒共振（n=1）是限制逃逸电子增长的有效机制，但天线对准和注入功率需满足一定条件；反常多普勒共振（n=-1）应避免；将螺旋波用于散射破裂后的逃逸电子束面临碰撞阻尼和真空间隙等工程挑战。

Abstract: Resonant wave-particle interactions between externally launched helicon waves (also known as whistler waves) and runaway electrons (REs) have been demonstrated on the DIII-D tokamak. In this work we extend the initial results reported in Choudhury, H. et al. Phys. Rev. Lett. 136, 025101 (2026) by exploring the effects of antenna alignment with the edge magnetic field, toroidal wave propagation direction, and coupled power on RE scattering in the quiescent RE experimental scenario. Two distinct experimental configurations have been investigated: one in which the antenna aligns well with the edge background magnetic field, known as the ideal antenna configuration, and one with misalignment, known as the non-ideal case. Previously, it had been found that helicon power in the ideal antenna configuration prevented RE growth despite the normalized toroidal electric field remaining high enough to drive exponential RE growth in the absence of helicon power. In this paper, we show that scattering via the normal Doppler resonance (n=1) effectively limits the growth of the RE population in both the ideal and non-ideal antenna configurations, with evidence of a power threshold in the latter case. In contrast, launching waves that favour the anomalous Doppler resonance (n=-1) is observed to enhance rather than reduce the RE population. In addition, fast magnetic measurements reveal rising-tones in the 30-60 MHz range during helicon-off periods, which are not observed prior to helicon power. Finally, the challenges of using launched helicon waves to scatter post-disruption RE beams are discussed. Collisional damping and a large vacuum gap between the plasma and antenna on the outboard side present significant obstacles to helicon waves propagating into the plasma core.

</details>


### [109] [Characterization of Numerical Dissipation in Simulations of Magnetohydrodynamic Turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22506)
*Yuyang Hua,Zhonghai Zhao,Bin Qiao*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个后验框架，用于直接从模拟数据中估计磁流体动力学（MHD）湍流中的数值耗散，无需先验假设。该框架在开源Python包PyMHD中实现，并应用于Alfvénic湍流、湍流小尺度发电机和MRI驱动的湍流模拟，系统地表征了数值耗散的各向异性和谱特性。结果表明，数值耗散主要耗散小尺度上由湍流级联传递的能量，但其谱特性与物理粘性和电阻率不同，不能简单地用有效耗散系数表示。此外，数值耗散继承了底层湍流的各向异性，甚至在某些情况下表现出反常的反耗散行为。该框架还能识别物理耗散在所有尺度上主导数值耗散的条件，为高保真度的天体物理MHD湍流模拟提供实用指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真度的MHD湍流模拟需要全面表征数值耗散，而现有方法通常依赖先验假设，缺乏直接从模拟数据中后验估计数值耗散的系统框架。

Method: 提出一个后验框架，通过开源Python包PyMHD实现，直接从MHD湍流模拟数据中估计数值耗散，无需先验假设。该框架应用于三种典型MHD湍流体系：Alfvénic湍流、湍流小尺度发电机和MRI驱动的湍流。

Result: 数值耗散主要耗散小尺度上湍流级联传递的能量；其谱特性与物理粘性和电阻率不同，不能简单用有效耗散系数表示；数值耗散继承底层湍流的各向异性，甚至可能表现出反常的反耗散行为；框架可识别物理耗散主导数值耗散的条件。

Conclusion: 该后验框架能系统地表征MHD湍流模拟中的数值耗散特性，为高保真度天体物理MHD湍流模拟提供实用指导，帮助研究者识别数值耗散可被忽略的参数空间。

Abstract: Comprehensive characterization of numerical dissipation is essential for high-fidelity simulations of magnetohydrodynamic (MHD) turbulence. In this work, we present an a posteriori framework for directly estimating numerical dissipation in MHD turbulence from simulation data without invoking a priori assumptions. Implemented in the open-source Python package PyMHD, the framework is applied to simulations of Alfvénic turbulence, turbulent small-scale dynamos, and MRI-driven turbulence, yielding a systematic characterization of the anisotropy and spectral properties of numerical dissipation across these regimes. The results indicate that numerical dissipation primarily dissipates energy transferred by the turbulent cascade at small scales, consistent with the conventional interpretation. However, its spectral properties are distinct from those of physical viscosity and resistivity, such that it cannot simply be represented by effective dissipation coefficients. In addition, numerical dissipation inherits the anisotropy of the underlying turbulence, and can even exhibit anomalous anti-dissipative behavior under certain circumstances. Moreover, this framework enables identification of the conditions under which physical dissipation dominates numerical dissipation across all scales, thereby providing practical guidance for achieving high-fidelity simulations of astrophysical MHD turbulence.

</details>


### [110] [Tearing Instability in Gyrotropic MHD: Effects of Equilibrium Pressure Anisotropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22535)
*Grzegorz Kowal,Gabriel L. Ferreira-Santos,Diego A. Falceta-Gonçalves*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了弱碰撞等离子体中压力各向异性对电阻撕裂模不稳定性的影响，推导了陀螺MHD框架下的线性理论，发现各向异性通过修改理想外区匹配参数和电阻内层动量平衡来改变撕裂模增长率，正Δβ₀抑制撕裂、负Δβ₀增强撕裂，且经典Lundquist数标度指数保持不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱碰撞等离子体在天体物理中普遍存在，能维持压力各向异性，但现有撕裂模理论大多假设各向同性平衡态，缺乏各向异性效应的系统性理论框架。

Method: 在非理想陀螺MHD框架下，对力-free Harris电流片进行线性理论分析，推导了理想外区匹配参数Δ'和上波数截止条件，改进了FKR分支和Coppi分支的色散关系，并通过匹配得到最大增长率标度律，最后用PSECAS特征值计算验证。

Result: 各向异性改变理想外区衰减率和匹配参数Δ'，上波数截止α_c=√(𝒜/ℛ₀)；最大增长率γ_maxτ_A~𝒜^{1/2}ℛ₀^{-1/4}S^{-1/2}，Lundquist数指数-1/2不变但前置因子依赖各向异性和β；正Δβ₀抑制撕裂模并展宽内层，负Δβ₀增强撕裂并使最快模向大波数移动。

Conclusion: 该工作明确了平衡压力各向异性如何同时修改理想外区匹配和电阻内层动力学，为理解天体物理等离子体中各向异性对磁重联的影响提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Weakly collisional plasmas are widespread in astrophysics and can sustain pressure anisotropy, yet most analytical tearing-mode scalings assume an isotropic equilibrium. We develop a linear theory of resistive tearing in nonideal gyrotropic MHD for a force-free Harris current sheet characterized by perpendicular plasma beta $β_0$ and parallel-minus-perpendicular beta difference $Δβ_0$. In the ideal outer region, anisotropy changes the far-field decay rate, the matching parameter $Δ'$, and the upper wavenumber cutoff for localized tearing, $α\equiv ka<α_c=\sqrt{\mathcal{A}/\mathcal{R}_0}$, with $\mathcal{A}=1-Δβ_0/2$ and $\mathcal{R}_0=1+\frac{1}{2}[(γ_\parallel+γ_\perp-2)β_0+γ_\parallelΔβ_0]$. In the resistive inner layer, anisotropy enters the leading momentum balance through $\mathcal{A}$. We derive modified FKR and Coppi branches and, by matching them at their crossover wavenumber, obtain $γ_{\max}τ_A\sim\mathcal{A}^{1/2}\mathcal{R}_0^{-1/4}S^{-1/2}$. Thus the classical Lundquist-number exponent is retained, while the prefactor depends on the equilibrium anisotropy, plasma beta, and gyrotropic closure. PSECAS eigenvalue calculations support the Coppi branch and are consistent with the FKR branch when a fitted finite-wavelength approximation for $Δ'$ is used. Within the localized-mode and pressure-positive domain, positive $Δβ_0$ generally suppresses tearing and broadens the inner layer, whereas negative $Δβ_0$ enhances growth and shifts the fastest mode to larger wavenumber. This work identifies how prescribed equilibrium pressure anisotropy modifies both ideal outer matching and resistive inner-layer dynamics in the gyrotropic-MHD regime.

</details>


### [111] [Evaluation of spatiotemporal tungsten density profiles using Unresolved Transition Arrays in the Large Helical Device](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22789)
*R. Nishimura,T. Oishi,I. Murakami,D. Kato,H. A. Sakaue,S. Gupta,H. Ohashi,C. Suzuki,M. Goto,Y. Kawamoto,R. T. Ishikawa,T. Kawate,K. Mukai,B. J. Peterson,H. Takahashi*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用大螺旋装置中的弹丸注入技术，通过空间分辨光谱仪和Slice & Stack方法，研究了电子温度低于1 keV、电子密度为10^19-10^20 m^-3的等离子体中钨的时空分布。钨密度由W^17+到W^27+的光子发射系数评估，并观测到钨从边缘烧蚀到核心扩散的过程，特别是一次NBI击穿事件后钨在核心区域的累积行为。辐射功率与辐射计测量结果进行了比对，验证了低至中等电荷态钨离子的原子数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低到中等电荷态的钨离子原子数据验证方面存在不足，需要通过对实际聚变等离子体中钨杂质输运行为的精确诊断来提供基准验证数据。

Method: 1) 在大螺旋装置(LHD)中使用弹丸注入技术引入钨杂质；2) 利用空间分辨光谱仪获取时空分布数据；3) 应用Slice & Stack方法重建191.7 Å谱线的发射率（该谱线属于90-250 Å范围内的未分辨跃迁阵列UTA）；4) 基于碰撞辐射模型评估W^17+至W^27+的光子发射系数，反演钨密度；5) 结合冷却因子数据集估算辐射功率并与辐射计测量对比。

Result: 钨弹丸从等离子体外侧注入后，先在边缘区域烧蚀，随后向等离子体内部扩散，符合弹丸注入实验的典型行为。在一次由NBI击穿触发的特定事件后，观察到钨在核心等离子体中发生累积。基于钨密度剖面和冷却因子估算的辐射功率与辐射计测量结果一致。

Conclusion: 该研究过程对于验证低至中等电荷态钨离子的原子数据具有重要价值，为聚变装置中钨杂质行为的实验诊断和原子物理建模提供了有效的基准方法。

Abstract: Tungsten spectroscopic studies have been conducted in the Large Helical Device with a pellet injection technique. Spatiotemporal profiles of tungsten density were evaluated using a space-resolved spectrometer, for plasmas with an electron temperature of below 1 keV and electron density of $10^{19}-10^{20}$ $m^{-3}$. Slice & Stack, a method for reconstructing emissivity, was applied to a line at 191.7 Å , which is a part of the Unresolved Transition Array (UTA) spectrum at 90-250 Å. Tungsten density was obtained using photon emission coefficients of $\mathrm{W}^{17+} - \mathrm{W}^{27+}$, evaluated from collisional-radiative model. The tungsten pellet injected from outside the plasma was first ablated in the edge plasma and subsequently diffused throughout the plasma. This behavior is typical of pellet injection experiments. Furthermore, after an event triggered by NBI breakdown, tungsten accumulated in the core plasma. The radiation power was estimated from the evaluated tungsten density profile and cooling factor dataset, and compared with bolometer measurement. This sequence of processes would be useful for validating atomic data of tungsten ions in low-to-intermediate charge states.

</details>


### [112] [Observation of stopping power reduction at strong ion-plasma coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23109)
*Yun Liu,Jieru Ren,Zhigang Deng,Wei Qi,Bubo Ma,Wenqing Wei,Shizheng Zhang,Xuyang Luo,Ziqian Zhao,Mingzhe Yang,Yifang Gao,Xueguang Ren,Jianxing Li,Dieter H. H. Hoffmann,Xing Wang,Zhongfeng Xu,Shaoyi Wang,Quanping Fan,Bo Cui,Weiwu Wang,Sixin Wu,Yue Yang,Zhurong Cao,Zongqing Zhao,Yuqiu Gu,Leifeng Cao,Bin He,Shaoping Zhu,Olga Rosmej,Rui Cheng,Guoqing Xiao,Weimin Zhou,Yongtao Zhao*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次在强耦合参数超过1的条件下实验研究了离子在稠密等离子体中的阻止本领，发现其低于标准线性介电响应或二元碰撞模型的预测，并与分子动力学结合量子修正的混合计算结果吻合，揭示了非线性屏蔽效应和量子效应的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强耦合等离子体中的离子阻止是恒星演化和聚变点火的关键，但超越线性极限的强耦合区域的阻止行为长期以来因实验难度巨大而难以研究。

Method: 使用激光加速产生的准单能碳离子（~583 keV/u, C⁵⁺）射入均匀、长寿命、表征完善的稠密等离子体靶（T_e≈17 eV, n_e≈4×10²⁰ cm⁻³），同时测量离子能量损失和电荷态演化以消除歧义。

Result: 强耦合条件下测量到的阻止本领低于标准线性介电响应或二元碰撞模型的预测，与分子动力学结合量子修正的混合计算结果高度吻合，证明了非线性多体屏蔽效应和量子效应的重要性。

Conclusion: 该工作为强耦合区域碰撞动力学建立了高保真实验基准，为惯性约束聚变和天体等离子体中的能量输运精确建模提供了关键见解。

Abstract: Ion stopping in dense plasma is crucial for stellar evolution and fusion ignition. However, its behavior in the strong ion-plasma coupling regime beyond the linear limit has long remained elusive, due to formidable experimental challenges. Here we report the first experimental investigation of ion stopping at an unprecedented coupling parameter exceeding unity, achieved by sending laser-accelerated short-pulse and intense quasi-monoenergetic carbon ions ($\sim$583 keV/u, C$^{5+}$) into a uniform, long-lived, well-characterized dense plasma target ($T_e$ $\approx$ 17 eV, $n_e$ $\approx$ 4$\times$10$^{20}$ cm$^{-3}$). By simultaneously measuring ion energy loss and charge-state evolution, we eliminated key experimental ambiguities arising from charge-state determination. Our results clearly show a reduction in stopping power compared with predictions from standard linear dielectric response or binary collision models, and they agree well with the hybrid calculation of molecular dynamics with quantum corrections. The importance of nonlinear screening effects arising from many-body interactions and quantum effects due to the wave nature of electrons was demonstrated at strong coupling. This work establishes a definitive high-fidelity experimental benchmark for collisional dynamics in the strong-coupling regime. It offers critical insight for accurate modeling of energy transport in inertial confinement fusion and astrophysical plasmas.

</details>


### [113] [Multi-objective Bayesian optimisation of a double-layer target for quasi-monoenergetic TNSA protons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23224)
*Chengqi-Zhang,Yang He,Mamat Ali Bake,Bai-Song Xie*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过六参数多目标贝叶斯优化，对碳氢双层靶进行靶后鞘层质子加速的二维PIC模拟研究，发现小半径氢层有助于维持准单能质子峰，并进行了三维验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索碳氢双层靶在靶后鞘层质子加速中的最优参数组合，特别是实现高能、准单能的质子束。

Method: 采用六参数（激光振幅a0、脉冲宽度τ、碳层厚度L1、氢层密度N2、氢层厚度L2、氢层半径rp）多目标贝叶斯优化，进行80次二维EPOCH模拟，以质子峰值能量、±10%能量窗内电荷分数及电荷量为优化目标。

Result: 峰值能量在64-71 MeV的最优解出现在a0=30、τ=45 fs、L1=0.3 μm、L2=30 nm、rp=0.15 μm附近；增加N2提高窗内电荷但增大带宽。三维验证显示峰值能量降至34.1 MeV，带宽缩窄至7.0%，但准单能峰得以保持。

Conclusion: 小半径氢层使质子源位于横向鞘场平坦中心区域，有利于维持准单能性；三维效应导致鞘场衰减更快和峰值能量饱和更早，从而降低峰值能量和带宽。

Abstract: We carry out a six-parameter multi-objective Bayesian optimisation of a carbon--hydrogen double-layer target for target-normal-sheath proton acceleration. The campaign consists of 80 two-dimensional EPOCH simulations with the laser amplitude $a_0$, pulse duration $τ$, carbon-layer thickness $L_1$, hydrogen-layer density $N_2$, hydrogen-layer thickness $L_2$ and hydrogen-layer radius $r_p$ as input variables. Each final proton spectrum is scored by the peak energy, the charge fraction inside a $\pm10\%$ peak-energy window and the charge in that window. Among the Pareto-set evaluations, the cases with peak energies between 64 and 71 MeV occur near $a_0=30$, $τ=45$ fs, $L_1=0.3\,μ{\rm m}$, $L_2=30$ nm and $r_p=0.15\,μ{\rm m}$. Along this branch, increasing $N_2$ raises the in-window charge and increases the bandwidth. The small rear-layer radius keeps the proton source within the flat central region of the transverse sheath field, where the accelerating field is nearly uniform. A 3D calculation is performed for the intermediate-density case $N_2=11.85\,n_c$, which balances bandwidth and in-window charge along this branch. The corresponding 2D spectrum has $E_{\rm peak}=67.4$ MeV and $ΔE/E=18.8\%$, whereas the 3D spectrum has $E_{\rm peak}=34.1$ MeV and $ΔE/E=7.0\%$. The lower 3D peak energy and narrower bandwidth are associated with an earlier decay of the rear-sheath field and an earlier saturation of the proton peak energy, and the quasi-monoenergetic peak is retained in 3D.

</details>


### [114] [On the accuracy of measurements of electron temperature by Thomson scattering diagnostic in the plasma core of the ITER tokamak](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23386)
*M. Yu. Kantor*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新评估了ITER托卡马克等离子体芯部汤姆逊散射诊断系统的电子温度测量精度。先前研究因错误应用汤姆逊散射截面而高估了光电产额，导致低估测量误差。修正分析表明：低背景辐射下，10%精度可维持至20 keV；高背景辐射下仅能维持至1 keV。为实现全温度范围10%精度，需将激光脉冲能量提升2-4倍或最低电子密度提高至6×10^19 m^-3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究因错误应用汤姆逊散射截面导致对光电产额的高估，进而显著低估了电子温度测量误差。因此需要重新评估ITER等离子体芯部汤姆逊散射诊断系统能否满足所需的测量精度要求。

Method: 基于修正后的光电产额（源自已发表数据），并纳入背景辐射影响，重新计算电子温度测量误差，评估原有诊断配置的可行性。

Result: 低背景辐射下，系统可在20 keV以内实现10%精度；高背景辐射下，精度仅能维持至1 keV。全温度范围达成10%精度需将激光脉冲能量提高2-4倍，或将最低电子密度提升至6×10^19 m^-3。

Conclusion: 原诊断配置在高温段无法满足精度要求，必须通过提升激光能量或提高最低电子密度来改进系统性能，才能在保持现有配置结构的前提下覆盖全温度范围。

Abstract: The ITER tokamak project includes a Thomson scattering diagnostic designed to measure electron temperature and density in the plasma core. The system is required to provide measurements over a wide temperature range while meeting stringent accuracy requirements. A previous study analyzed the errors of electron temperature measurements to assess the feasibility of these requirements. The analysis concluded that the central electron temperature could be measured with the required accuracy of 10% for temperatures up to 40 keV at a minimum electron density of 3*10^19 m^-3. However, those results were based on an overestimation of the number of photoelectrons generated in detectors by the scattered radiation due to the incorrect application of the Thomson scattering cross-section. As a consequence, the temperature measurement errors were significantly underestimated. In the present work, the accuracy of electron temperature measurements in the ITER plasma core is reassessed using the corrected photoelectron yield derived from published data and incorporating the effects of background radiation. The revised analysis shows that the proposed diagnostic system can achieve the required accuracy of 10% for temperatures up to 20 keV at low plasma background radiation, whereas under high background radiation this accuracy can only be maintained up to 1 keV. To achieve the 10% accuracy across the full temperature range while preserving the current diagnostic configuration, either the energy of the probing laser pulse must be increased by a factor of 2-4 or the least electron density must be raised to 6*10^19 m^-3.

</details>


### [115] [Disambiguation of magnetic sensors in ITER](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23427)
*M. J. Hole,S. D. Pinches,G. Vayakis,S. C. McIntosh,O. Hoenen,P. Abreu*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于匈牙利算法的优化策略，用于解决ITER装置中约500个磁传感器线圈的极性识别与接线错误校正问题。该方法通过组合极向和环向非对称主动线圈的励磁，利用Biot-Savart计算磁场解，将问题建模为符号分配问题，实现O(N³)的高效求解。在规则阵列中，信噪比低至50时仍可完成识别，计算仅需2秒；第一壁线圈重构置信度>0.97，第二壁线圈通过引入极向场线圈后置信度从>0.15提升至>0.59。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ITER装置约500个磁传感器分布在一壁，存在线圈极性接线错误及信号不匹配问题。现有策略需要最多48次无等离子体放电的组合编程，耗时且复杂。需要更高效的替代方案来加速ITER的调试过程。

Method: 组合极向和环向非对称主动线圈励磁，通过Biot-Savart计算所有主动线圈在传感器处的磁场解。将问题数学化为符号分配问题，利用AI辅助编码识别问题结构，采用匈牙利算法（O(N³)）替代暴力穷举（O(2^N N!)）进行优化搜索。

Result: 规则阵列中，信噪比低至50时可完成随机连接和极性线圈的识别，计算2秒；第一壁线圈重构置信度>0.97；第二壁线圈初始置信度>0.15，加入极向场主动线圈后提升至>0.59。

Conclusion: 该方法可缩短ITER调试时间，并有望推广至其他环形磁约束聚变装置。

Abstract: ITER will possess approximately 500 magnetic sensors (mainly measuring poloidal flux) distributed across the first wall. The coils are at known locations but the matching signals not necessarily known. There may also be mistakes in the wiring of the coil polarity. The existing strategy to disambiguate coils uses combinatoric programming of poloidal field coil waveforms of up to 48 discharges of plasma-less operation. An alternate strategy explored in this work is the energisation of a combination of both poloidal and toroidally asymmetric active coils, and Biot-Savart computation of the field solution from all active coils at the sensor coils. A direct brute force permutation of all $N$ coil combinations scales as $O(2^N N!) $ which is intractable for $N>10$. The mathematically formulated optimisation problem was analysed using AI-assisted coding tools, which identified the problem structure as a signed assignment problem and suggested a Hungarian-algorithm-based optimisation strategy, which scales as $O(N^3)$. This search algorithm, when embedded into the magnetic-diagnostic identification problem, was able to disambiguate randomly connected and polarised coils in a regularly spaced array in the ITER first wall (where the the coils are located) down to a signal-to-noise ratio of 50. The computation took 2 seconds. Reconstruction of the actual coil positions in the ITER first wall was achieved high confidence, $C>0.97$. Reconstruction of the second wall poloidal flux coils, which comprised multiple arrays at near constant $φ$ (each of which is regularly spaced in $θ$) had a much lower confidence, of $C > 0.15$. By adding the active poloidal field coils to the combined cost function, the confidence increased to $C > 0.59$. This provides the opportunity to reduce the commissioning time of ITER, and is a strategy that could be tested on other toroidal magnetic confinement devices.

</details>


### [116] [Detachment dynamics and disturbance rejection in the TCV X-Point Target divertor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23432)
*M. Winkel,K. Verhaegh,B. Kool,K. Lee,M. Carpita,A. Perek,R. Morgan,G. Derks,O. Février,C. Theiler,D. Brida,M. van Berkel,the TCV team,the EUROfusion tokamak exploitation team*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了TCV托卡马克上X点靶板（XPT）偏滤器构型在D₂加料、N₂注入和ECRH功率调制下的动态响应与扰动抑制能力，并与单零（SN）偏滤器进行对比。结果表明，XPT在其次级X点处具有固有的扰动抑制能力，有利于被动缓冲功率排出控制器难以有效管理的扰动，但同时也给监测靠近次级X点的脱离态带来挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索X点靶板（XPT）偏滤器构型这一替代方案在动态响应和扰动抑制方面的特性，并与标准单零（SN）偏滤器进行对比，以评估其在未来聚变装置中的应用潜力。

Method: 采用系统辨识方法，利用多正弦扰动信号测量TCV托卡马克上Ohmic和辅助加热L模条件下，D₂加料、N₂注入以及ECRH功率调制对脱离态的动态响应。

Result: XPT在其次级X点处对所有扰动场景均表现出固有的扰动抑制能力；而在其次级X点上游，XPT与SN的脱离态动态响应相似。

Conclusion: XPT的扰动抑制能力可被动缓冲功率排出控制器难以有效管理的扰动，具有实际应用价值；但同时给靠近次级X点的脱离态监测带来挑战。

Abstract: The X-Point Target divertor is an alternative divertor configuration with a secondary X-point in its divertor volume. In this work, we investigate the dynamic response and disturbance rejection capacity of the XPT configuration on the TCV tokamak, comparing it to a single null (SN) divertor. We employ a system identification approach using multi-sine perturbations to measure the dynamic response of the detached state in both Ohmic and auxiliary-heated L-mode scenarios upon D$_2$ fuelling, N$_2$ seeding and Electron Resonance Cyclotron Heating (ECRH) power modulations. We demonstrate an inherent disturbance rejection capacity of the XPT at its secondary X-point compared to a SN configuration for all perturbation scenarios. Upstream of its secondary X-point, the dynamic response of the detached state between the XPT and SN appears similar. The disturbance rejection capacity of the XPT could be highly beneficial for passively buffering disturbances that cannot be effectively managed by power exhaust controllers. At the same time, it presents a challenge for monitoring the detached state close to the secondary x-point.

</details>


<div id='physics.acc-ph'></div>

# physics.acc-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [Role of the Local Electric-Field in High-Field Conditioning of DC Electrodes:Numerical and Experimental Insights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21259)
*Victoria Madeleine Bjelland,William Lee Millar,Morten Kildemo,Walter Wuensch*

Main category: physics.acc-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在高场强器件中，表面电场空间分布如何影响 conditioning（通过受控电场施加逐步提高电压耐受能力的过程）。研究发现，暴露于更高电场的区域更容易发生击穿，但这种趋势被更快的 conditioning 速率所抵消。通过蒙特卡洛模拟与脉冲直流电极系统的实验对比，验证了这一相互作用机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Understanding how conditioning varies within a device when there is a spatial variation in the surface electric field, to explain observed breakdown locations and gain insights into the mechanisms underlying conditioning and breakdown.

Method: 结合蒙特卡洛模拟与实验结果。实验采用脉冲直流高场电极对，表面电场径向变化，通过双摄像头三角定位记录每次击穿的位置。

Result: 更高电场区域击穿倾向更大，但同时 conditioning 速率更快，两者相互作用决定了击穿位置分布。模拟结果与实验观测一致。

Conclusion: 该相互作用机制解释了 conditioning 过程中击穿位置的分布规律，为理解 conditioning 和击穿的内在机理提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Conditioning, the progressive increase of voltage-holding through the controlled application of fields, is an important and widely used process for bringing high-field and high-voltage devices up to their full operating parameters. Here, a study is presented on how conditioning can vary within a device, specifically, when there is a spatial variation in the surface electric field. What has been observed in high-field pulsed direct-current electrodes and radio-frequency structures is that locations exposed to higher fields exhibit a greater tendency to breakdown, but this increase is counteracted by an increased conditioning rate. This interplay explains the observed breakdown locations and provides important insights into the mechanisms underlying both conditioning and breakdown. This study combines Monte Carlo simulations with experimental results from pairs of high-field electrodes with a radially varying surface electric field. Results are presented from a high-field pulsed DC system, in which the position of each breakdown during conditioning was recorded by triangulation using a pair of cameras, and the results are compared with Monte Carlo simulations.

</details>


### [118] [Quantum noninvasive three-component beam-spin polarimetry in the Hadron Storage Ring of the Electron-Ion Collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22265)
*Frank Rathmann*

Main category: physics.acc-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于SQUID的非侵入式极化仪，利用质子束团的集体磁偶极矩而非散射过程，在EIC的HSR中实现全极化矢量(Px, Py, Pz)的连续监测与高精度极化幅度测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的单粒子散射极化测量无法获取纵向极化分量Pz（受宇称守恒限制），且需要破坏性测量。为此，本文提出一种非侵入式、可连续监测且能测量全极化矢量的SQUID极化仪方案，以满足EIC高精度极化束运行需求。

Method: 利用DC SQUID拾取束流极化进动信号：三个拾取通道（cosine-θ和sine-θ鞍形环测横向分量，同轴轴向梯度仪测纵向分量）重构全极化矢量。静态模式（默认）连续非侵入监测三个分量；动态模式通过纵向kicker将小部分极化倾转到水平面产生自由感应衰减信号，经多次相同相位锁定的tip-π-echo-restore循环后匹配滤波相干累加，实现高精度极化幅度测量。

Result: 对于倾斜角α=30 mrad、极化度P=0.7、有效自旋调谐展宽σ_{ν_s}^{eff}=10^{-3}（相干时间~2 ms），达到δP/P=1%所需的积分时间在注入时为~18 s，平顶时为~5 min。每次循环的极化损失约为O(α^2/π^2)~10^{-4}，对整个1%测量可忽略。

Conclusion: 该SQUID极化仪可实现对EIC HSR质子束的全极化矢量非侵入式测量，并可扩展至氘核和³He束流，还可应用于存储环电偶极矩搜索实验。

Abstract: We propose a noninvasive SQUID-based polarimeter for the polarized proton beam in the Electron-Ion Collider (EIC) Hadron Storage Ring (HSR), exploiting the collective magnetic dipole moment of the bunches rather than scattering. The six-snake HSR lattice has synchronous-particle spin tune $ν_s = 1/2$, placing the in-plane spin-precession signal at half the revolution frequency ($\sim$39 kHz), in the DC SQUID band. Three pickup channels (cosine-$θ$ and sine-$θ$ saddle loops for the transverse components, a coaxial axial gradiometer for the longitudinal one) reconstruct the full polarization vector $(P_x, P_y, P_z)$ in two complementary modes. Static mode, the default for continuous noninvasive monitoring, reads all three components: $P_y$ at the revolution frequency and the residual in-plane components at $ν_s f_\mathrm{rev}$, bunch by bunch over an hours-long fill, including $P_z$, inaccessible to single-spin scattering polarimetry by parity conservation. Dynamic mode gives a precise polarization-magnitude measurement: a longitudinal kicker tips a small fraction of the polarization into the horizontal (ring) plane to produce a free-induction-decay (FID) signal, and many phase-locked tip-$π$-echo-restore cycles are summed coherently via a matched filter across all bunches, with $\mathcal{O}(α^2/π^2) \sim 10^{-4}$ loss per cycle, negligible over a full $δP/P = 1\%$ measurement. For tipping angle $α= 30$ mrad, polarization $P = 0.7$, and effective rms spin-tune spread $σ_{ν_s}^\mathrm{eff} = 10^{-3}$ (coherence time $\sim$2 ms), the integration time to reach $δP/P = 1\%$ is about 18 s at injection and 5 min at flattop. The architecture extends to deuteron and $^3$He beams via species-specific spin-magnetic factors, with applications to storage-ring EDM searches.

</details>


### [119] [Powder Spreading and Layer Deposition in Metal Powder Bed Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22989)
*Antonello Astarita*

Main category: physics.acc-ph

TL;DR: 本章系统研究了粉末床熔融技术中粉末铺展与层沉积的机理，分析了粉末特性、工艺参数与设备架构对粉末床质量的影响，并探讨了DEM模拟、粉末循环利用及数据驱动优化等先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粉末铺展和层沉积是PBF技术的基础阶段，对工艺稳定性和最终构件质量具有决定性影响，然而粉末床形成机制复杂，涉及多因素耦合作用，亟需系统性理解与量化表征。

Method: 通过分析粒度分布、形貌、内聚力、流动性的影响，结合层厚、刮刀速度、环境条件等工艺参数，并采用离散元方法（DEM）建模粉末铺展行为，量化粉末床质量指标。

Result: 粉末床作为工艺状态变量，其堆积密度、表面覆盖率、有效层厚和空间均匀性等特征直接影响能量吸收、熔池稳定性、缺陷形成和力学性能。

Conclusion: 粉末工程在先进金属增材制造中日益重要，未来方向包括过程监控、数字孪生和数据驱动的优化策略，以及粉末再利用与全生命周期管理。

Abstract: Powder spreading and layer deposition are fundamental stages of Powder Bed Fusion (PBF) technologies and play a critical role in determining process stability and final component quality. This chapter examines the mechanisms governing powder-bed formation, highlighting the interactions between powder characteristics, process parameters, and machine architecture. Particular attention is devoted to the influence of particle size distribution, morphology, cohesion, flowability, layer thickness, recoater velocity, and environmental conditions on powder-bed quality. The resulting powder-bed is discussed as a process state variable whose characteristics, including packing density, surface coverage, effective layer thickness, and spatial homogeneity, directly affect energy absorption, melt-pool stability, defect formation, and mechanical performance. The chapter also reviews the application of the Discrete Element Method (DEM) for modelling powder spreading phenomena and quantifying powder-bed quality metrics. Finally, the role of powder reuse, lifecycle management, and future developments involving process monitoring, digital twins, and data-driven optimization strategies is discussed, emphasizing the growing importance of powder engineering in advanced metal additive manufacturing.

</details>


### [120] [Transverse Emittance and Emittance Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23013)
*Gero Kube*

Main category: physics.acc-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了横向发射度的迹空间描述，将其与Courant-Snyder形式体系和二阶矩束流矩阵相联系，并阐述了利用轮廓测量、光阑、漂移段、四极铁扫描和横向偏转结构等诊断技术将不可直接测量的角度或切片信息转换为可观测束流尺寸的实验逻辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 横向发射度是带电粒子束的关键品质因子，连接微观相空间分布与宏观加速器性能，需要系统性的诊断方法将其从不可直接测量的量转换为可观测的束流尺寸。

Method: 通过迹空间描述框架，结合Courant-Snyder形式体系和二阶矩束流矩阵，利用轮廓测量、光阑、漂移段、四极铁扫描和横向偏转结构等实验技术进行诊断。

Result: 建立了从不可直接测量的角度或切片信息到可观测束流尺寸的转换方法体系。

Conclusion: 横向发射度的测量依赖于将抽象的相空间信息通过多种诊断技术转化为可观测的束流尺寸参数。

Abstract: Transverse emittance is one of the central figures of merit for charged-particle beams because it connects the microscopic phase-space distribution to macroscopic accelerator performance. This report introduces the trace-space description of emittance, relates it to the Courant-Snyder formalism and the second-moment beam matrix, and then follows the experimental logic behind common diagnostics. Emphasis is placed on how profile measurements, masks, drifts, quadrupole scans, and transverse deflecting structures convert otherwise inaccessible angular or slice information into measurable beam sizes.

</details>


### [121] [Monte Carlo_Based Beam Dynamics Study of a Compact Linear Accelerator for Localized X_ray Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23366)
*Hossein Emami,Hossein Afarideh*

Main category: physics.acc-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过蒙特卡洛束流动力学模拟，研究了全长0.5米的紧凑型直线加速器（LINAC）在60 keV和300 keV两种工作模式下的性能对比。结果表明，60 keV低能模式受集体效应影响显著，导致发射度增长和晕圈形成；而300 keV模式具有更好的束流刚性、相空间保持能力和更紧凑的靶点尺寸。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑型电子直线加速器因其小占地面积和良好束流控制能力，在局部X射线产生和能量可控辐照方面需求日益增长，因此需要研究不同能量模式下的束流传输特性。

Method: 采用基于蒙特卡洛框架的粒子追踪方法，包含RF腔加速、简化四极磁聚焦和空间电荷诱导扩散效应，在相同晶格结构下对60 keV和300 keV两种模式进行横向和纵向相空间演化的对比分析。

Result: 60 keV模式下集体效应显著，发射度增长明显且产生晕圈；300 keV模式具有更高的束流刚性，相空间保持更好，靶点束斑更紧凑。

Conclusion: 能量是影响紧凑型LINAC束流质量的关键参数，较高能量（300 keV）模式在束流品质和靶点形成方面优于低能量（60 keV）模式，为紧凑型加速器设计提供了能量选择的依据。

Abstract: Compact electron linear accelerators are being increasingly investigated as enabling platforms for localized X-ray generation and energy-controlled irradiation, due to the demand for a reduced footprint and improved beam control. In this work, a conceptual compact LINAC architecture with a fixed total length of 0.5 m is investigated through comprehensive Monte Carlo beam dynamics simulations, focusing on the comparative performance of two operating modes delivering electron beams at 60 keV and 300 keV, respectively. The study aims to isolate energy-dependent beam transport effects within an identical lattice configuration, emphasizing transverse and longitudinal phase-space evolution, emittance growth, beam envelope stability, and target spot formation. Particle tracking is performed using a statistical Monte Carlo framework incorporating RF cavity acceleration, simplified quadrupole focusing, and space-charge-induced diffusion effects. Transverse and longitudinal phase spaces are analyzed at multiple locations along the accelerator, and key beam quality metrics are extracted at the exit. The results demonstrate that low-energy operation at 60 keV is strongly influenced by collective effects, leading to pronounced emittance growth and halo formation, whereas the 300 keV mode exhibits significantly enhanced beam rigidity, improved phase space preservation, and a compact beam spot at the target.

</details>


### [122] [SuperCond-GNN: Scalable Graph Neural Network Surrogate for Superconducting Circuit Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23548)
*Nandana Menon,Giorgio Vallone*

Main category: physics.acc-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出SuperCond-GNN，一种基于图神经网络的代理模型，用于预测高温超导磁体中的电压分布。该模型将HTS磁体建模为集总元件等效电路并映射为图表示，通过消息传递GNN学习电路拓扑、材料属性和工作电流与电响应之间的关系。在包含多达10个带材的带材堆栈上验证，平均MAPE为4.3%，并评估了基于基尔霍夫电流定律的物理信息正则化效果以及零样本推理和少样本微调的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电路求解器在大规模设计空间探索、电流共享分析和实时磁体监测等下游应用中计算效率低、可扩展性差，需要一种更快速可扩展的替代方案来预测HTS磁体的电压分布。

Method: 将HTS磁体建模为集总元件等效电路并映射为图结构，利用消息传递GNN学习电路拓扑、材料属性和工作电流与电压响应的映射关系。采用电路仿真数据训练，并引入基于基尔霍夫电流定律的物理信息正则化。通过零样本推理和少样本微调评估对未见拓扑的泛化能力。

Result: 在多达10个带材的带材堆栈电路中，代理模型平均MAPE为4.3%。物理信息正则化提升了模型性能，且模型展现出对未见拓扑的零样本和少样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该图神经网络框架是拓扑无关的，可自然扩展到更复杂的HTS电缆和磁体配置，为传统电路求解器提供了一种可扩展的替代方案，适用于设计空间探索、电流共享分析和实时磁体监测等下游应用。

Abstract: This paper presents SuperCond-GNN, a graph neural network-based surrogate model for predicting the voltage distribution in high-temperature superconducting (HTS) magnets. HTS magnets are modeled as lumped-element equivalent circuits and mapped onto graph representations, enabling message passing GNNs to learn the electrical response as a function of circuit topology, material properties, and operating current. As a proof of concept, tape stacks of up to 10 tapes are considered across a range of circuit topologies and operating conditions. The surrogate is trained on data generated from circuit simulations and achieves a mean MAPE of 4.3 % within the prescribed design space. The predicted nodal voltages enable fast and scalable inference of current redistribution and local operating conditions across a wide range of circuit configurations. The effect of incorporating physics-informed regularization via Kirchhoff's current law is also evaluated, and generalizability to unseen topologies is assessed through zero-shot inference and few-shot fine-tuning. While demonstrated on tape stack circuits, the graph-based framework is topology-agnostic and naturally extensible to more complex HTS cable and magnet configurations, offering a scalable alternative to conventional circuit solvers for downstream applications such as design space exploration, current sharing analysis, and real-time magnet monitoring.
